STAGE.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=False)
record = {
    "status": "PREPARING",
    "project_backup": str(SAVED),
    "gpu": torch.cuda.get_device_name(0),
    "torch": str(torch.__version__),
    "training_invoked": False,
    "settings_changed": False,
    "job": job,
    "restored_archive_sha256": manifest["archive_sha256"]
}
started = time.perf_counter()

try:
    # Restore only mismatched packages. Keep the existing GPU PyTorch build.
    pins = {
        "transformers": "4.57.1",
        "tokenizers": "0.22.1",
        "peft": "0.17.1",
        "trl": "0.27.2",
        "bitsandbytes": "0.50.2"
    }
    constraints = STAGE / "constraints.txt"
    constraints.write_text(
        "\n".join(f"{name}=={version}" for name, version in pins.items())
        + "\ntorch==2.11.0+cu130\nhuggingface_hub<1\n"
    )
    requirements = [
        Requirement(line.split("#", 1)[0].strip())
        for line in (ROOT / "requirements.txt").read_text().splitlines()
        if line.split("#", 1)[0].strip()
    ]
    requirements += [
        Requirement(f"{name}=={version}") for name, version in pins.items()
    ]
    requirements += [Requirement("huggingface_hub>=0.27,<1")]

    def mismatches():
        missing = []
        for requirement in requirements:
            if requirement.marker and not requirement.marker.evaluate():
                continue
            try:
                installed = metadata.version(requirement.name)
            except metadata.PackageNotFoundError:
                missing.append(str(requirement))
                continue
            if installed not in requirement.specifier:
                missing.append(f"{requirement}: found {installed}")
        return sorted(set(missing))

    problems = mismatches()
    record["package_restoration_needed"] = bool(problems)
    if problems:
        print("Restoring pinned packages for this GPU runtime:", flush=True)
        print("\n".join(problems), flush=True)
        install = subprocess.run(
            [
                sys.executable, "-m", "pip", "install",
                "-r", str(ROOT / "requirements.txt"),
                "-c", str(constraints)
            ],
            cwd=ROOT, text=True, capture_output=True
        )
        (STAGE / "installation.log").write_text(
            install.stdout + "\n" + install.stderr
        )
        if install.returncode:
            print("\n".join(
                (install.stdout + install.stderr).splitlines()[-40:]
            ))
            raise RuntimeError("Package restoration failed; paste the output.")
    else:
        print("Pinned packages already match; installation skipped.", flush=True)
        (STAGE / "installation.log").write_text(
            "Skipped: required versions already installed.\n"
        )

    assert not mismatches(), mismatches()
    assert metadata.version("torch") == "2.11.0+cu130", (
        "GPU PyTorch was changed unexpectedly."
    )
    record["packages"] = {
        name: metadata.version(name) for name in pins
    }

    # Copy only policy files needed for training; reward files stay on Drive.
    MODELS = Path(job["local_models_dir"])
    SOURCE_MODELS = Path(job["drive_models_dir"])
    manifest_path = SOURCE_MODELS / "model_manifest.json"
    assert sha(manifest_path) == job["model_manifest_sha256"]
    model_manifest = json.loads(manifest_path.read_text())
    policy = model_manifest["policy"]
    assert (policy["model_id"], policy["revision"]) == (
        "Qwen/Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct",
        "989aa7980e4cf806f80c7fef2b1adb7bc71aa306"
    )

    source = (SOURCE_MODELS / policy["directory"]).resolve()
    destination = (MODELS / policy["directory"]).resolve()
    assert SOURCE_MODELS.resolve() in source.parents
    assert MODELS.resolve() in destination.parents
    destination.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    print(
        "Copying saved policy files from Drive to local disk; no model download...",
        flush=True
    )
    for index, (name, digest) in enumerate(policy["sha256"].items(), 1):
        origin = (source / name).resolve()
        target = (destination / name).resolve()
        assert source in origin.parents and destination in target.parents
        if not target.exists():
            target.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            shutil.copyfile(origin, target)
        assert sha(target) == digest, f"Prepared policy file differs: {name}"
        print(
            f"Policy file {index}/{len(policy['sha256'])}: {name}",
            flush=True
        )

    local_manifest = MODELS / "model_manifest.json"
    if local_manifest.exists():
        assert sha(local_manifest) == job["model_manifest_sha256"]
    else:
        shutil.copyfile(manifest_path, local_manifest)
    assert sha(local_manifest) == job["model_manifest_sha256"]

    # The existing training script validates the approved configuration/data,
    # trains the full budget, verifies reload, and verifies its Drive backup.
    record["status"] = "TRAINING_INVOKED"
    record["training_invoked"] = True
    (STAGE / "launch_record.json").write_text(json.dumps(record, indent=2))
    print(
        "STARTING OFFICIAL STANDARD TRAINING: "
        "1,500 pairs; beta 0.1; 94 updates.",
        flush=True
    )

    environment = dict(
        os.environ, PYTHONUNBUFFERED="1", HF_HUB_OFFLINE="1"
    )
    command = [sys.executable, "-u", *job["command_arguments"]]

    with (STAGE / "training_console.log").open(
        "w", encoding="utf-8"
    ) as log:
        process = subprocess.Popen(
            command, cwd=ROOT, env=environment,
            stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
            text=True, bufsize=1, start_new_session=True
        )
        try:
            for line in process.stdout:
                print(line, end="", flush=True)
                log.write(line)
                log.flush()
            return_code = process.wait()
        except BaseException:
            if process.poll() is None:
                os.killpg(process.pid, signal.SIGINT)
                process.wait()
            raise

    record["exit_code"] = return_code
    assert return_code == 0, (
        "Training failed; paste the output. Any catchable training failure "
        "is recorded and backed up by the training script."
    )

    checkpoint = ROOT / "outputs/task1_dpo/standard"
    trained = json.loads((checkpoint / "run_record.json").read_text())
    assert trained["status"] == "COMPLETE_RELOAD_VERIFIED"
    assert (
        trained["completed_pairs"], trained["completed_updates"]
    ) == (1500, 94)

    backup_name = f"standard_{trained['started_at']}_{trained['status']}"
    backup_receipt = json.loads(
        (TRAINING_BACKUP / (backup_name + "_backup_receipt.json")).read_text()
    )
    assert backup_receipt["status"] == "BACKUP_VERIFIED"
    saved_checkpoint = Path(backup_receipt["destination"])
    assert sha(saved_checkpoint / "run_record.json") == sha(
        checkpoint / "run_record.json"
    )
    record.update({
        "status": "STANDARD_TRAINING_COMPLETE",
        "completed_pairs": 1500,
        "completed_updates": 94,
        "checkpoint_backup": str(saved_checkpoint)
    })

except BaseException as error:
    record["status"] = "FAILED"
    record["error"] = f"{type(error).__name__}: {error}"
    raise

finally:
    record["launcher_wall_seconds"] = time.perf_counter() - started
    (STAGE / "launch_record.json").write_text(json.dumps(record, indent=2))
    evidence_copy = EVIDENCE / STAGE.name
    hashes = {
        str(p.relative_to(STAGE)): sha(p)
        for p in STAGE.rglob("*") if p.is_file()
    }
    shutil.copytree(STAGE, evidence_copy)
    copied = {
        str(p.relative_to(evidence_copy)): sha(p)
        for p in evidence_copy.rglob("*") if p.is_file()
    }
    assert copied == hashes, (
        "Launch evidence backup failed; keep the runtime connected."
    )
    print("Launch log/evidence saved and verified:", evidence_copy, flush=True)
    print("Flushing Drive writes before GPU release...", flush=True)
    drive.flush_and_unmount(timeout_ms=120000)
    print("DRIVE_WRITES_FLUSHED", flush=True)

print("STANDARD_TRAINING_COMPLETE")
print("Verified checkpoint on Drive:", record["checkpoint_backup"])
print("Save the notebook, then disconnect and delete the GPU runtime before further discussion.")

# CPU only: inspect saved failure evidence; no training or model loading.
import hashlib
import json
import math
from datetime import datetime
from pathlib import Path
from zoneinfo import ZoneInfo
import torch
from google.colab import drive

assert not torch.cuda.is_available(), "Switch to a CPU runtime first."
drive.mount("/content/drive")

BASE = Path("/content/drive/MyDrive/ATML-Assignment-2")
FAILED = BASE / "training_runs/standard_20261004_135137_662295_FAILED"
RECEIPT = FAILED.with_name(FAILED.name + "_backup_receipt.json")

receipt = json.loads(RECEIPT.read_text())
assert receipt["status"] == "BACKUP_VERIFIED"
assert Path(receipt["destination"]) == FAILED
verified = {}

def read_verified(relative):
    data = (FAILED / relative).read_bytes()
    digest = hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()
    assert digest == receipt["sha256"][relative], f"Saved file changed: {relative}"
    verified[relative] = digest
    return data.decode("utf-8")

record = json.loads(read_verified("run_record.json"))
events = [
    json.loads(line)
    for line in read_verified("training_trace.jsonl").splitlines()
    if line.strip()
]
dpo_source = read_verified("source_snapshot/task1_dpo/dpo.py")
read_verified("source_snapshot/task1_dpo/runtime.py")

for name in ("dpo.py", "runtime.py"):
    key = "task1_dpo/" + name
    assert verified["source_snapshot/" + key] == record["source_sha256"][key]
assert record["status"] == "FAILED"

updates = [e for e in events if e["event"] == "optimizer_update"]
failures = [e for e in events if e["event"] == "nonfinite_gradients"]
assert len(failures) == 1, "Expected one gradient-failure event; paste this message if different."

failure = failures[0]
window = [
    e for e in events
    if e["event"] == "microbatch" and e["update"] == failure["update"]
]
last = updates[-1] if updates else {"update": 0, "seen_pairs": 0}
assert record["completed_updates"] == last["update"]
assert record["completed_pairs"] == last["seen_pairs"]

bad_names = [
    record["trainable_names"][i]
    for i in failure["parameter_indices"]
]
all_batches = [e for e in events if e["event"] == "microbatch"]
numeric_keys = (
    "policy_chosen_logp", "policy_rejected_logp",
    "reference_chosen_logp", "reference_rejected_logp",
    "reference_adjusted_margin",
)

summary = {
    "run_status": record["status"],
    "error": record["error"],
    "completed_optimizer_updates": record["completed_updates"],
    "pairs_in_completed_updates": record["completed_pairs"],
    "failed_update": failure["update"],
    "microbatches_in_failed_update": len(window),
    "pairs_in_failed_update": sum(e["pairs"] for e in window),
    "all_logged_losses_finite": all(
        math.isfinite(e["mean_loss"]) for e in all_batches
    ),
    "all_logged_response_scores_and_margins_finite": all(
        math.isfinite(v)
        for e in all_batches for key in numeric_keys for v in e[key]
    ),
    "adapter_tensors_with_nonfinite_gradients": len(bad_names),
    "scaler_settings": record["scaler_settings"],
    "autocast": record["autocast"],
    "trainable_dtypes": record["trainable_dtypes"],
    "run_wall_seconds": record["total_wall_seconds"],
}
formula_lines = [
    line.strip() for line in dpo_source.splitlines()
    if "margin = policy_margin - ref_margin" in line
    or "logits = float(beta) * margin" in line
    or '"preference_accuracy"' in line
]
report = {
    "status": "FAILED_RUN_DIAGNOSTIC_ONLY",
    "failed_run_directory": str(FAILED),
    "backup_receipt_sha256": hashlib.sha256(RECEIPT.read_bytes()).hexdigest(),
    "verified_input_sha256": verified,
    "summary": summary,
    "last_three_completed_updates": updates[-3:],
    "failed_window_microbatches": window,
    "nonfinite_gradient_parameter_names": bad_names,
    "saved_objective_formula_lines": formula_lines,
    "cause": "Unresolved; no GPU replay performed.",
    "training_or_settings_changed": False,
    "precision_change_approval": "Pending; no correction applied.",
}

stamp = datetime.now(ZoneInfo("Asia/Karachi")).strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S_%f")
evidence = (
    BASE / "setup_evidence/20261003_161419_807248"
    / ("standard_failure_diagnostic_" + stamp)
)
evidence.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=False)
payload = json.dumps(report, indent=2, allow_nan=False).encode("utf-8")
saved = evidence / "diagnostic.json"
saved.write_bytes(payload)
assert saved.read_bytes() == payload, "Diagnostic save verification failed."

print(json.dumps(summary, indent=2))
print("\nLast completed updates (update / gradient norm / scale):")
for e in updates[-3:]:
    print(
        e["update"], e["gradient_norm_before_clipping"],
        e["scale_before"], "->", e["scale_after"]
    )
print("\nFailed update batches (microbatch / loss / original prompt IDs):")
for e in window:
    print(e["microbatch"], round(e["mean_loss"], 6), e["prompt_ids"])
print("\nSaved accuracy calculation:")
for line in formula_lines:
    print(line)

print("\nCPU_FAILURE_DIAGNOSTIC_OK")
print("Verified diagnostic saved:", evidence)
print("GPU remains off. No model weights loaded or settings changed.")

# CPU only: apply the approved scale change, document it, and save a retry snapshot.
import ast
import difflib
import hashlib
import json
import shutil
import stat
import tempfile
import zipfile
from datetime import datetime
from pathlib import Path, PurePosixPath
from zoneinfo import ZoneInfo
import torch
from google.colab import drive

assert not torch.cuda.is_available(), "Keep CPU selected for this preparation cell."
drive.mount("/content/drive")

BASE = Path("/content/drive/MyDrive/ATML-Assignment-2")
ROOT = Path("/content/ATML-Assignment-2")
OLD = BASE / "runtime_backups/20261004_133443_697113"
FAILED = BASE / "training_runs/standard_20261004_135137_662295_FAILED"
DIAGNOSTIC = BASE / "setup_evidence/20261003_161419_807248/standard_failure_diagnostic_20261004_140644_711479/diagnostic.json"

def sha(path):
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    with Path(path).open("rb") as handle:
        for chunk in iter(lambda: handle.read(2**20), b""):
            digest.update(chunk)
    return digest.hexdigest()

def save_json(path, value):
    payload = json.dumps(value, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False, allow_nan=False)
    path.write_text(payload, encoding="utf-8")
    assert path.read_text(encoding="utf-8") == payload

manifest = json.loads((OLD / "backup_manifest.json").read_text())
assert manifest["status"] == "BACKUP_VERIFIED"
assert manifest["archive_root"] == ROOT.name
expected = manifest["file_sha256"]
assert len(expected) == 271
assert set(expected) == set(manifest["included_files"])
assert sha(OLD / "project.zip") == manifest["archive_sha256"]

print("Restoring/verifying the saved project on CPU...", flush=True)
if not ROOT.exists():
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(
        prefix="atml_retry_restore_", dir="/content"
    ) as temporary:
        with zipfile.ZipFile(OLD / "project.zip") as archive:
            entries = archive.infolist()
            names = {
                str(PurePosixPath(ROOT.name) / name)
                for name in expected
            }
            assert len(entries) == len(names)
            assert {e.filename for e in entries} == names
            for entry in entries:
                path = PurePosixPath(entry.filename)
                assert not path.is_absolute() and ".." not in path.parts
                assert path.parts[0] == ROOT.name and not entry.is_dir()
                assert not stat.S_ISLNK(entry.external_attr >> 16)
            archive.extractall(temporary)
        restored = Path(temporary) / ROOT.name
        for name, digest in expected.items():
            assert sha(restored / name) == digest, f"Restore differs: {name}"
        shutil.move(str(restored), str(ROOT))

for name, digest in expected.items():
    assert (ROOT / name).is_file() and sha(ROOT / name) == digest, (
        f"Existing file differs; preserve it and paste this output: {name}"
    )

receipt_path = Path("/content/ATML_PA2_setup_receipt.json")
receipt_bytes = (OLD / receipt_path.name).read_bytes()
assert not receipt_path.exists() or receipt_path.read_bytes() == receipt_bytes
receipt_path.write_bytes(receipt_bytes)
EVIDENCE = Path(json.loads(receipt_bytes)["backup_directory"])
assert EVIDENCE.is_dir()

failure_receipt = json.loads(
    FAILED.with_name(FAILED.name + "_backup_receipt.json").read_text()
)
assert failure_receipt["status"] == "BACKUP_VERIFIED"
assert Path(failure_receipt["destination"]) == FAILED

diagnostic_bytes = DIAGNOSTIC.read_bytes()
diagnostic = json.loads(diagnostic_bytes)
assert diagnostic["status"] == "FAILED_RUN_DIAGNOSTIC_ONLY"
assert diagnostic["summary"]["completed_optimizer_updates"] == 18
assert diagnostic["summary"]["failed_update"] == 19

for name, digest in diagnostic["verified_input_sha256"].items():
    assert failure_receipt["sha256"][name] == digest
    assert sha(FAILED / name) == digest

relative = "task1_dpo/train.py"
target = ROOT / relative
before_bytes = target.read_bytes()
before = before_bytes.decode("utf-8")
assert expected[relative] == failure_receipt["sha256"][
    "source_snapshot/" + relative
]

replacements = {
    "init_scale=65536.": "init_scale=1024.",
    '"init_scale": 65536.': '"init_scale": 1024.',
}
after = before
for old, new in replacements.items():
    assert after.count(old) == 1, f"Expected one occurrence: {old}"
    after = after.replace(old, new, 1)

after_bytes = after.encode("utf-8")
compile(after, str(target), "exec")

restored_source = after
for old, new in replacements.items():
    restored_source = restored_source.replace(new, old, 1)
assert restored_source == before, "Unexpected source change."

calls = [
    n for n in ast.walk(ast.parse(after))
    if isinstance(n, ast.Call)
    and isinstance(n.func, ast.Attribute)
    and n.func.attr == "GradScaler"
]
assert len(calls) == 1
arguments = {
    k.arg: ast.literal_eval(k.value)
    for k in calls[0].keywords
}
assert arguments == {
    "enabled": True,
    "init_scale": 1024.,
    "growth_factor": 2.,
    "backoff_factor": .5,
    "growth_interval": 2000,
}

stamp = datetime.now(ZoneInfo("Asia/Karachi")).strftime(
    "%Y%m%d_%H%M%S_%f"
)
STAGE = ROOT / "results/setup" / ("gradient_scale1024_" + stamp)
STAGE.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=False)

(STAGE / "original_train.py").write_bytes(before_bytes)
target.write_bytes(after_bytes)
assert target.read_bytes() == after_bytes
(STAGE / "patched_train.py").write_bytes(after_bytes)
(STAGE / "source_change.diff").write_text(
    "".join(difflib.unified_diff(
        before.splitlines(keepends=True),
        after.splitlines(keepends=True),
        fromfile="original/task1_dpo/train.py",
        tofile="approved/task1_dpo/train.py",
    ))
)

for name, digest in expected.items():
    if name != relative:
        assert sha(ROOT / name) == digest, (
            f"Unrelated original file changed: {name}"
        )
(STAGE / "failure_diagnostic.json").write_bytes(diagnostic_bytes)

approval = {
    "status": "USER_APPROVED_CHANGE_APPLIED_ON_CPU",
    "user_approval": "okay approved, as long as our theory is recorded as well the change sure go ahead",
    "recorded_at_Asia_Karachi": stamp,
    "conditions": [
        "standard", "beta_003", "beta_010",
        "beta_030", "length_balanced",
    ],
    "change": {
        "initial_gradient_scale_before": 65536.,
        "initial_gradient_scale_after": 1024.,
    },
    "theory": "Unconfirmed FP16 backward overflow, possibly amplified by scale 65536; trace does not identify the exact operation or prove causation.",
    "reasoning": "1024 reduces temporary amplification 64-fold; unscaling retains the intended optimizer update in exact arithmetic, while rounding can differ.",
    "tradeoff": "Lower amplification can increase underflow susceptibility; 1024 is a conservative trial setting, not a guarantee.",
    "failure_evidence": str(FAILED),
    "diagnostic_evidence": str(DIAGNOSTIC),
    "failure_summary": diagnostic["summary"],
    "source_before_sha256": hashlib.sha256(before_bytes).hexdigest(),
    "source_after_sha256": sha(target),
    "source_change_scope": "Only GradScaler init_scale and its matching run-record value.",
    "all_other_original_snapshot_files_unchanged": True,
    "retry": "Original pinned base, fresh adapters/optimizer/scaler, seed6304, full budgets, distinct output; preserve failed evidence.",
    "retained": "FP16 base; FP32 LoRA; no autocast; growth2/backoff0.5/interval2000; unscale before clip1; strict stop on nonfinite gradients; all data/limits/betas/optimizer/generation settings.",
    "verification_scope": "Exact source change, syntax and original-file hashes; no new test suite or model computation.",
    "gpu_retry_started": False,
    "root_cause_confirmed": False,
    "successful_fix_confirmed": False,
    "reference": "https://docs.pytorch.org/docs/2.11/amp.html#gradient-scaling",
}
save_json(ROOT / "docs/task1_gradient_scale1024_approval.json", approval)

notes = """# Approved Task 1 numerical change: initial gradient scale 1024

The first standard attempt completed 18 updates / 288 pairs. Update 19
(eight microbatches / 16 pairs) produced nonfinite gradients in eight adapter
tensors despite finite logged forward losses and scores. The strict guard
stopped before applying that update. No completed checkpoint is claimed.
The saved objective and preference accuracy match the PA reference-adjusted margin.

Theory: FP16 backward overflow, potentially amplified by scale 65536.
This is an unconfirmed hypothesis; the trace does not locate the exact operation.
FP32 adapters still receive gradients through FP16 model calculations.

The user explicitly approved lowering initial GradScaler from 65536 to 1024
and a fresh restart, conditional on recording the theory and change.
This reduces temporary amplification 64-fold. Unscaling preserves the intended
update in exact arithmetic; numerical rounding can differ, and lower scaling
can increase underflow susceptibility. Successful full-budget training remains unverified.

The shared training entrypoint applies 1024 to all five fresh DPO conditions.
Only the constructor value and its matching run-record value changed.
All data, 4096 limits, beta values, seed, LoRA, optimizer, accumulation,
clipping, precision and generation settings remain as previously approved.
The strict finite-gradient guard remains: no skipped pairs or automatic precision changes.

The failed attempt and original script are preserved. Standard retries from
the pinned original base with fresh adapters/optimizer/scaler and seed6304,
for all 1500 pairs / 94 updates. Later evaluation must use the completed retry's
actual adapter path, recorded in the retry job, rather than the failed output.
The paired JSON records approval, hashes, theory, tradeoff and evidence paths.
"""
(ROOT / "docs/task1_gradient_scale1024.md").write_text(
    notes, encoding="utf-8"
)
save_json(STAGE / "validation.json", approval)

old_job = json.loads((OLD / "standard_training_job.json").read_text())
assert (
    old_job["pairs"], old_job["epochs"],
    old_job["beta"], old_job["optimizer_updates"]
) == (1500, 1, .1, 94)
assert sha(
    Path(old_job["drive_models_dir"]) / "model_manifest.json"
) == old_job["model_manifest_sha256"]

SAVED = BASE / "runtime_backups" / stamp
SAVED.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=False)
output = ROOT / "outputs/task1_dpo" / (
    "standard_retry_scale1024_" + stamp
)
assert not output.exists()

job = dict(old_job)
job.update({
    "status": "PREPARED_RETRY_NOT_STARTED",
    "project_backup": str(SAVED),
    "retry_output": str(output),
    "initial_gradient_scale": 1024.,
    "prior_failed_attempt": str(FAILED),
    "approved_settings_changed": True,
    "approved_change": "Initial GradScaler 65536 -> 1024 only; fresh full-budget restart.",
    "train_source_sha256": sha(target),
    "approval_file": "docs/task1_gradient_scale1024_approval.json",
    "project_job_file": "docs/task1_standard_retry_job.json",
    "command_arguments": old_job["command_arguments"] + [
        "--output", str(output)
    ],
    "next": "Restore this saved snapshot on A100, restore pinned packages and saved policy files, then run the fresh standard retry once.",
})
save_json(ROOT / job["project_job_file"], job)

copy = EVIDENCE / STAGE.name
shutil.copytree(STAGE, copy)
stage_hashes = {
    str(p.relative_to(STAGE)): sha(p)
    for p in STAGE.rglob("*") if p.is_file()
}
assert stage_hashes == {
    str(p.relative_to(copy)): sha(p)
    for p in copy.rglob("*") if p.is_file()
}

included, excluded = [], []
for path in sorted(ROOT.rglob("*")):
    if not path.is_file():
        continue
    rel = path.relative_to(ROOT)
    if any(
        part in {".git", "__pycache__", ".cache", ".venv"}
        for part in rel.parts
    ):
        continue
    if rel.parts[0] in {"checkpoints", "cached"} and path.suffix != ".md":
        excluded.append(str(rel))
        continue
    included.append(path)

file_hashes = {
    str(p.relative_to(ROOT)): sha(p)
    for p in included
}
print(
    f"Saving the revised project: {len(included)} files; model weights stay on Drive...",
    flush=True,
)
with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(
    prefix="atml_retry_backup_", dir="/content"
) as temporary:
    local_zip = Path(temporary) / "project.zip"
    with zipfile.ZipFile(
        local_zip, "w", zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED, compresslevel=1
    ) as archive:
        for path in included:
            archive.write(
                path, str(Path(ROOT.name) / path.relative_to(ROOT))
            )
    shutil.copyfile(local_zip, SAVED / "project.zip")
    archive_sha = sha(local_zip)
    assert sha(SAVED / "project.zip") == archive_sha

    with zipfile.ZipFile(SAVED / "project.zip") as archive:
        assert archive.testzip() is None
        for name, digest in file_hashes.items():
            assert hashlib.sha256(
                archive.read(str(Path(ROOT.name) / name))
            ).hexdigest() == digest

    assert file_hashes == {
        str(p.relative_to(ROOT)): sha(p) for p in included
    }

shutil.copyfile(receipt_path, SAVED / receipt_path.name)
assert sha(SAVED / receipt_path.name) == sha(receipt_path)

new_manifest = {
    "status": "BACKUP_VERIFIED",
    "archive_root": ROOT.name,
    "archive_sha256": archive_sha,
    "included_files": list(file_hashes),
    "file_sha256": file_hashes,
    "excluded_downloadable_course_assets": excluded,
    "drive_models_dir": old_job["drive_models_dir"],
    "official_training_started": True,
    "retry_training_started": False,
    "prior_failed_attempt": str(FAILED),
    "initial_gradient_scale": 1024.,
}
save_json(SAVED / "backup_manifest.json", new_manifest)
save_json(SAVED / "standard_training_job.json", job)

print("GRADIENT_SCALE_CHANGE_BACKUP_OK")
print("Approved initial scale: 65536 -> 1024; shared by all five DPO conditions.")
print("Theory recorded as UNCONFIRMED; exact two-value change and syntax verified.")
print("Verified project backup:", SAVED)
print("Verified change evidence:", copy)
print("Fresh standard output:", output)
print("Full budget: 1500 pairs; beta0.1; 94 updates. Failed attempt preserved.")
print("Keep CPU selected. No weights loaded; no test suites rerun; no training started.")

from pathlib import Path, PurePosixPath
from datetime import datetime
from zoneinfo import ZoneInfo
from importlib import metadata
from pip._vendor.packaging.requirements import Requirement
import hashlib, json, os, shutil, signal, stat
import subprocess, sys, tempfile, time, zipfile
import torch
from google.colab import drive

print("PyTorch:", torch.__version__, "CUDA:", torch.version.cuda, flush=True)
assert torch.cuda.is_available(), "Select A100 GPU, save, and reconnect first."
print("GPU:", torch.cuda.get_device_name(0), flush=True)
assert "A100" in torch.cuda.get_device_name(0), (
    "Another GPU was assigned; paste the output before continuing."
)
assert torch.cuda.get_device_properties(0).total_memory / 2**30 >= 39
assert str(torch.__version__) == "2.11.0+cu130", (
    "PyTorch differs from the validated runtime; paste the output."
)
assert torch.version.cuda == "13.0"
drive.mount("/content/drive")

ROOT = Path("/content/ATML-Assignment-2")
RECEIPT = Path("/content/ATML_PA2_setup_receipt.json")
SAVED = Path(
    "/content/drive/MyDrive/ATML-Assignment-2/runtime_backups/20261004_142324_585826"
)

def sha(path):
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    with Path(path).open("rb") as handle:
        for chunk in iter(lambda: handle.read(2**20), b""):
            digest.update(chunk)
    return digest.hexdigest()

manifest = json.loads((SAVED / "backup_manifest.json").read_text())
job = json.loads((SAVED / "standard_training_job.json").read_text())
assert manifest["status"] == "BACKUP_VERIFIED"
assert manifest["archive_root"] == ROOT.name
assert manifest["official_training_started"] is True
assert manifest["retry_training_started"] is False
assert job["status"] == "PREPARED_RETRY_NOT_STARTED"
assert manifest["initial_gradient_scale"] == job["initial_gradient_scale"] == 1024.
assert job["approved_settings_changed"] is True
assert job["project_backup"] == str(SAVED)
assert (
    job["pairs"], job["epochs"], job["beta"], job["optimizer_updates"]
) == (1500, 1, 0.1, 94)

expected = manifest["file_sha256"]
assert set(expected) == set(manifest["included_files"])
assert len(expected) == 279
archive_path = SAVED / "project.zip"
assert sha(archive_path) == manifest["archive_sha256"], "Saved ZIP changed."

print("Restoring the verified 279-file retry project...", flush=True)
if ROOT.exists():
    differences = [
        name for name, digest in expected.items()
        if not (ROOT / name).is_file() or sha(ROOT / name) != digest
    ]
    assert not differences, (
        f"Existing project differs; preserve it and paste: {differences[:10]}"
    )
else:
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(
        prefix="atml_gpu_restore_", dir="/content"
    ) as temporary:
        with zipfile.ZipFile(archive_path) as archive:
            entries = archive.infolist()
            names = {
                str(PurePosixPath(ROOT.name) / name) for name in expected
            }
            assert len(entries) == len(names)
            assert {entry.filename for entry in entries} == names
            for entry in entries:
                path = PurePosixPath(entry.filename)
                assert not path.is_absolute() and ".." not in path.parts
                assert path.parts[0] == ROOT.name and not entry.is_dir()
                assert not stat.S_ISLNK(entry.external_attr >> 16)
            archive.extractall(temporary)
        restored = Path(temporary) / ROOT.name
        for name, digest in expected.items():
            assert sha(restored / name) == digest, f"Restore differs: {name}"
        shutil.move(str(restored), str(ROOT))

receipt_bytes = (SAVED / RECEIPT.name).read_bytes()
assert not RECEIPT.exists() or RECEIPT.read_bytes() == receipt_bytes
RECEIPT.write_bytes(receipt_bytes)
EVIDENCE = Path(json.loads(receipt_bytes)["backup_directory"])
assert EVIDENCE.is_dir()

assert job["project_job_file"] == "docs/task1_standard_retry_job.json"
assert json.loads((ROOT / job["project_job_file"]).read_text()) == job
assert (
    sha(ROOT / "task1_dpo/train.py")
    == job["train_source_sha256"]
    == expected["task1_dpo/train.py"]
)
approval = json.loads((ROOT / job["approval_file"]).read_text())
assert approval["status"] == "USER_APPROVED_CHANGE_APPLIED_ON_CPU"
assert approval["change"] == {
    "initial_gradient_scale_before": 65536.,
    "initial_gradient_scale_after": 1024.,
}
assert approval["source_after_sha256"] == job["train_source_sha256"]

TRAINING_BACKUP = Path(job["training_backup_root"])
TRAINING_BACKUP.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
checkpoint = Path(job["retry_output"])
assert checkpoint == ROOT / (
    "outputs/task1_dpo/standard_retry_scale1024_20261004_142324_585826"
)
assert not checkpoint.exists(), (
    "Retry output already exists; preserve it and paste the output."
)
prior_failed = Path(job["prior_failed_attempt"])
assert prior_failed == TRAINING_BACKUP / (
    "standard_20261004_135137_662295_FAILED"
)
prior_record = json.loads((prior_failed / "run_record.json").read_text())
assert prior_record["status"] == "FAILED"
assert prior_record["completed_updates"] == 18
assert sorted(
    p for p in TRAINING_BACKUP.glob("standard_*") if p.is_dir()
) == [prior_failed], (
    "Another standard attempt exists; preserve it and paste the output."
)
expected_arguments = [
    "-m", "task1_dpo.train", "--config", "configs/dpo.yaml",
    "--run-name", "standard",
    "--models-dir", "/content/ATML_PA2_models",
    "--backup-root", str(TRAINING_BACKUP),
    "--output", str(checkpoint),
]
assert job["command_arguments"] == expected_arguments

stamp = datetime.now(ZoneInfo("Asia/Karachi")).strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S_%f")
STAGE = ROOT / "results/setup" / f"gpu_standard_retry_scale1024_{stamp}"
STAGE.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=False)
record = {
    "status": "PREPARING",
    "project_backup": str(SAVED),
    "gpu": torch.cuda.get_device_name(0),
    "torch": str(torch.__version__),
    "training_invoked": False,
    "launcher_changed_ML_settings": False,
    "initial_gradient_scale": 1024.,
    "fresh_restart": True,
    "theory_status": "UNCONFIRMED",
    "approval": approval,
    "job": job,
    "restored_archive_sha256": manifest["archive_sha256"],
}
started = time.perf_counter()

try:
    shutil.copyfile(
        ROOT / job["approval_file"], STAGE / "theory_and_approval.json"
    )
    shutil.copyfile(
        ROOT / "docs/task1_gradient_scale1024.md",
        STAGE / "theory_and_change.md",
    )

    # Restore required package versions without replacing GPU PyTorch.
    pins = {
        "transformers": "4.57.1", "tokenizers": "0.22.1",
        "peft": "0.17.1", "trl": "0.27.2", "bitsandbytes": "0.50.2",
    }
    constraints = STAGE / "constraints.txt"
    constraints.write_text(
        "\n".join(f"{name}=={version}" for name, version in pins.items())
        + "\ntorch==2.11.0+cu130\nhuggingface_hub<1\n"
    )
    requirements = [
        Requirement(line.split("#", 1)[0].strip())
        for line in (ROOT / "requirements.txt").read_text().splitlines()
        if line.split("#", 1)[0].strip()
    ]
    requirements += [
        Requirement(f"{name}=={version}") for name, version in pins.items()
    ]
    requirements += [Requirement("huggingface_hub>=0.27,<1")]

    def mismatches():
        missing = []
        for requirement in requirements:
            if requirement.marker and not requirement.marker.evaluate():
                continue
            try:
                installed = metadata.version(requirement.name)
            except metadata.PackageNotFoundError:
                missing.append(str(requirement))
                continue
            if installed not in requirement.specifier:
                missing.append(f"{requirement}: found {installed}")
        return sorted(set(missing))

    problems = mismatches()
    record["package_restoration_needed"] = bool(problems)
    if problems:
        print("Restoring pinned packages:", flush=True)
        print("\n".join(problems), flush=True)
        install = subprocess.run(
            [
                sys.executable, "-m", "pip", "install",
                "-r", str(ROOT / "requirements.txt"),
                "-c", str(constraints),
            ],
            cwd=ROOT, text=True, capture_output=True,
        )
        (STAGE / "installation.log").write_text(
            install.stdout + "\n" + install.stderr
        )
        if install.returncode:
            print("\n".join(
                (install.stdout + install.stderr).splitlines()[-40:]
            ))
            raise RuntimeError("Package restoration failed; paste the output.")
    else:
        print("Pinned packages match; installation skipped.", flush=True)
        (STAGE / "installation.log").write_text(
            "Skipped: required versions already installed.\n"
        )
    assert not mismatches(), mismatches()
    assert metadata.version("torch") == "2.11.0+cu130"
    record["packages"] = {
        name: metadata.version(name) for name in pins
    }

    # Restore saved policy files locally. Reward files stay on Drive.
    MODELS = Path(job["local_models_dir"])
    SOURCE_MODELS = Path(job["drive_models_dir"])
    manifest_path = SOURCE_MODELS / "model_manifest.json"
    assert sha(manifest_path) == job["model_manifest_sha256"]
    model_manifest = json.loads(manifest_path.read_text())
    policy = model_manifest["policy"]
    assert (policy["model_id"], policy["revision"]) == (
        "Qwen/Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct",
        "989aa7980e4cf806f80c7fef2b1adb7bc71aa306",
    )
    source = (SOURCE_MODELS / policy["directory"]).resolve()
    destination = (MODELS / policy["directory"]).resolve()
    assert SOURCE_MODELS.resolve() in source.parents
    assert MODELS.resolve() in destination.parents
    destination.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    print("Restoring saved policy files; no model download...", flush=True)
    for index, (name, digest) in enumerate(policy["sha256"].items(), 1):
        origin = (source / name).resolve()
        target = (destination / name).resolve()
        assert source in origin.parents and destination in target.parents
        if not target.exists():
            target.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            shutil.copyfile(origin, target)
        assert sha(target) == digest, f"Policy file differs: {name}"
        print(
            f"Policy file {index}/{len(policy['sha256'])}: {name}",
            flush=True,
        )

    local_manifest = MODELS / "model_manifest.json"
    if local_manifest.exists():
        assert sha(local_manifest) == job["model_manifest_sha256"]
    else:
        shutil.copyfile(manifest_path, local_manifest)
    assert sha(local_manifest) == job["model_manifest_sha256"]

    # Fresh training, full budget, existing reload and backup verification.
    record["status"] = "TRAINING_INVOKED"
    record["training_invoked"] = True
    (STAGE / "launch_record.json").write_text(
        json.dumps(record, indent=2)
    )
    print(
        "STARTING FRESH STANDARD RETRY: "
        "1,500 pairs; beta 0.1; 94 updates; scale 1024.",
        flush=True,
    )
    environment = dict(
        os.environ, PYTHONUNBUFFERED="1", HF_HUB_OFFLINE="1"
    )
    command = [sys.executable, "-u", *job["command_arguments"]]
    with (STAGE / "training_console.log").open(
        "w", encoding="utf-8"
    ) as log:
        process = subprocess.Popen(
            command, cwd=ROOT, env=environment,
            stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
            text=True, bufsize=1, start_new_session=True,
        )
        try:
            for line in process.stdout:
                print(line, end="", flush=True)
                log.write(line)
                log.flush()
            return_code = process.wait()
        except BaseException:
            if process.poll() is None:
                os.killpg(process.pid, signal.SIGINT)
                process.wait()
            raise

    record["exit_code"] = return_code
    assert return_code == 0, (
        "Training failed; paste the output. Catchable training failures "
        "are recorded and backed up by the training script."
    )

    trained = json.loads((checkpoint / "run_record.json").read_text())
    assert trained["status"] == "COMPLETE_RELOAD_VERIFIED"
    assert (
        trained["completed_pairs"], trained["completed_updates"]
    ) == (1500, 94)
    assert trained["scaler_settings"] == {
        "enabled": True, "init_scale": 1024.,
        "growth_factor": 2., "backoff_factor": .5,
        "growth_interval": 2000,
    }
    assert trained["source_sha256"]["task1_dpo/train.py"] == (
        job["train_source_sha256"]
    )
    assert json.loads(
        (checkpoint / "reload_verification.json").read_text()
    )["status"] == "PASS"

    backup_name = f"standard_{trained['started_at']}_{trained['status']}"
    backup_receipt = json.loads(
        (TRAINING_BACKUP / (backup_name + "_backup_receipt.json")).read_text()
    )
    assert backup_receipt["status"] == "BACKUP_VERIFIED"
    saved_checkpoint = Path(backup_receipt["destination"])
    assert sha(saved_checkpoint / "run_record.json") == sha(
        checkpoint / "run_record.json"
    )
    record.update({
        "status": "STANDARD_RETRY_TRAINING_COMPLETE",
        "completed_pairs": 1500, "completed_updates": 94,
        "checkpoint_backup": str(saved_checkpoint),
    })

except BaseException as error:
    record["status"] = "FAILED"
    record["error"] = f"{type(error).__name__}: {error}"
    raise

finally:
    record["launcher_wall_seconds"] = time.perf_counter() - started
    (STAGE / "launch_record.json").write_text(
        json.dumps(record, indent=2)
    )
    evidence_copy = EVIDENCE / STAGE.name
    hashes = {
        str(p.relative_to(STAGE)): sha(p)
        for p in STAGE.rglob("*") if p.is_file()
    }
    shutil.copytree(STAGE, evidence_copy)
    copied = {
        str(p.relative_to(evidence_copy)): sha(p)
        for p in evidence_copy.rglob("*") if p.is_file()
    }
    assert copied == hashes, (
        "Launch evidence backup failed; keep the runtime connected."
    )
    print("Launch evidence saved and verified:", evidence_copy, flush=True)
    print("Flushing Drive writes before GPU release...", flush=True)
    drive.flush_and_unmount(timeout_ms=120000)
    print("DRIVE_WRITES_FLUSHED", flush=True)

print("STANDARD_RETRY_TRAINING_COMPLETE")
print("Verified checkpoint on Drive:", record["checkpoint_backup"])
print("Save notebook; disconnect and delete GPU runtime; paste the output.")

# CPU only: record completed training and prepare approved evaluation jobs.
import hashlib
import json
from collections import Counter
from datetime import datetime
from pathlib import Path
from zoneinfo import ZoneInfo
import torch
from google.colab import drive

assert not torch.cuda.is_available(), (
    "Disconnect/delete the GPU runtime and select CPU first."
)
drive.mount("/content/drive")

BASE = Path("/content/drive/MyDrive/ATML-Assignment-2")
PROJECT = BASE / "runtime_backups/20261004_142324_585826"
CHECKPOINT = BASE / "training_runs/standard_20261004_143635_293670_COMPLETE_RELOAD_VERIFIED"
FAILED = BASE / "training_runs/standard_20261004_135137_662295_FAILED"
EVIDENCE = BASE / "setup_evidence/20261003_161419_807248"

def sha(path):
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    with Path(path).open("rb") as handle:
        for chunk in iter(lambda: handle.read(2**20), b""):
            digest.update(chunk)
    return digest.hexdigest()

def save_json(path, value):
    payload = json.dumps(value, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False, allow_nan=False)
    path.write_text(payload, encoding="utf-8")
    assert path.read_text(encoding="utf-8") == payload

receipt_path = CHECKPOINT.with_name(
    CHECKPOINT.name + "_backup_receipt.json"
)
receipt = json.loads(receipt_path.read_text())
assert receipt["status"] == "BACKUP_VERIFIED"
assert Path(receipt["destination"]) == CHECKPOINT

names = (
    "run_record.json", "reload_verification.json",
    "adapter_config.json", "adapter_model.safetensors",
)
hashes = {name: sha(CHECKPOINT / name) for name in names}
assert all(hashes[name] == receipt["sha256"][name] for name in names)

record = json.loads((CHECKPOINT / "run_record.json").read_text())
reload_check = json.loads(
    (CHECKPOINT / "reload_verification.json").read_text()
)
assert record["status"] == "COMPLETE_RELOAD_VERIFIED"
assert (record["completed_pairs"], record["completed_updates"]) == (1500, 94)
assert record["plan"]["name"] == "standard"
assert record["plan"]["beta"] == .1
assert record["scaler_settings"] == {
    "enabled": True, "init_scale": 1024.,
    "growth_factor": 2., "backoff_factor": .5, "growth_interval": 2000,
}
assert reload_check["status"] == "PASS"
assert reload_check["held_out_examples_used"] is False

training = record["training"]
assert training["update_pair_counts"] == [16] * 93 + [12]
assert Counter(training["ordered_prompt_ids"]) == Counter(
    record["selected_prompt_ids"]
)
assert record["frozen_base_before_sha256"] == record["frozen_base_after_sha256"]

manifest = json.loads((PROJECT / "backup_manifest.json").read_text())
assert manifest["status"] == "BACKUP_VERIFIED"
assert len(manifest["included_files"]) == 279
assert all(
    manifest["file_sha256"][name] == digest
    for name, digest in record["source_sha256"].items()
)

old_receipt = json.loads(
    FAILED.with_name(FAILED.name + "_backup_receipt.json").read_text()
)
assert old_receipt["status"] == "BACKUP_VERIFIED"
assert sha(FAILED / "run_record.json") == old_receipt["sha256"]["run_record.json"]
old = json.loads((FAILED / "run_record.json").read_text())
assert old["status"] == "FAILED"
assert old["scaler_settings"]["init_scale"] == 65536.

same_initialization = all(
    record[key] == old[key]
    for key in (
        "seed", "effective_config", "selected_prompt_ids",
        "initial_adapter_sha256", "frozen_base_before_sha256",
    )
)
assert same_initialization, (
    "Initialization/configuration differs; paste the output for review."
)
assert set(record["source_sha256"]) == set(old["source_sha256"])
assert all(
    record["source_sha256"][name] == digest
    for name, digest in old["source_sha256"].items()
    if name != "task1_dpo/train.py"
)

summary = {
    "status": "STANDARD_TRAINING_COMPLETE_RELOAD_AND_BACKUP_VERIFIED",
    "completed_pairs": 1500,
    "optimizer_updates": 94,
    "final_update_pairs": 12,
    "training_minutes": record["training_wall_seconds"] / 60,
    "training_mean_loss": training["training_mean_loss"],
    "training_preference_accuracy": training["training_preference_accuracy"],
    "initial_gradient_scale": 1024.,
    "same_initialization_as_failed_attempt": same_initialization,
    "frozen_base_unchanged": True,
    "reload_verified": True,
    "peak_allocated_VRAM_GiB": record["peak_allocated_VRAM_GiB"],
    "peak_reserved_VRAM_GiB": record["peak_reserved_VRAM_GiB"],
    "metrics_scope": "Training diagnostics only; held-out evaluation has not started.",
    "theory_outcome": "The fresh scale1024 run completed the entire budget. This is consistent with the overflow hypothesis, but does not prove the original failure's exact cause.",
    "checkpoint": str(CHECKPOINT),
    "checkpoint_file_sha256": hashes,
    "prior_failed_attempt": str(FAILED),
}

old_job = json.loads((PROJECT / "standard_training_job.json").read_text())
stamp = datetime.now(ZoneInfo("Asia/Karachi")).strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S_%f")
STAGE = EVIDENCE / ("standard_complete_evaluation_jobs_" + stamp)
STAGE.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=False)

local_adapter = "/content/ATML_PA2_adapters/standard_completed_scale1024"
evaluation_backup = str(BASE / "evaluation_runs")
jobs = []

for condition in ("standard", "sft"):
    output = (
        f"/content/ATML-Assignment-2/results/task1_dpo/"
        f"{condition}/policy_{stamp}"
    )
    arguments = [
        "-m", "task1_dpo.evaluate",
        "--config", "configs/dpo.yaml",
        "--name", condition, "--phase", "policy",
        "--models-dir", old_job["local_models_dir"],
        "--output", output,
        "--backup-root", evaluation_backup,
    ]
    if condition == "standard":
        arguments += ["--adapter", local_adapter]

    jobs.append({
        "condition": condition,
        "phase": "policy",
        "output": output,
        "command_arguments": arguments,
        "pair_pools": {
            "dpo_standard_eval": 300, "dpo_length_eval": 246
        } if condition == "standard" else {},
        "generation_pools": {
            "dpo_standard_eval": 300, "word_limit_prompts": 10
        },
    })

bundle = {
    "status": "PREPARED_POLICY_EVALUATION_NOT_STARTED",
    "project_backup": str(PROJECT),
    "project_archive_sha256": manifest["archive_sha256"],
    "completed_checkpoint": str(CHECKPOINT),
    "checkpoint_file_sha256": hashes,
    "local_adapter": local_adapter,
    "drive_models_dir": old_job["drive_models_dir"],
    "local_models_dir": old_job["local_models_dir"],
    "model_manifest_sha256": old_job["model_manifest_sha256"],
    "evaluation_backup_root": evaluation_backup,
    "jobs": jobs,
    "ML_settings_changed": False,
    "scope": "Previously approved full evaluation pools and untouched SFT baseline; separate fresh processes and verified backups. Reward scoring follows using saved responses.",
}

save_json(STAGE / "training_completion.json", summary)
save_json(STAGE / "policy_evaluation_jobs.json", bundle)
(STAGE / "checkpoint_backup_receipt.json").write_bytes(receipt_path.read_bytes())
assert (
    (STAGE / "checkpoint_backup_receipt.json").read_bytes()
    == receipt_path.read_bytes()
)
save_json(STAGE / "evidence_sha256.json", {
    "training_completion.json": sha(STAGE / "training_completion.json"),
    "policy_evaluation_jobs.json": sha(STAGE / "policy_evaluation_jobs.json"),
    "checkpoint_backup_receipt.json": sha(receipt_path),
})

print(json.dumps(summary, indent=2))
print("\nSTANDARD_RESULT_AND_EVALUATION_JOBS_OK")
print("Saved training result and theory outcome:", STAGE)
print(
    "Next GPU job: standard 546 held-out pairs + 310 generated answers; "
    "baseline 310 answers."
)
print(
    "Reward scoring follows; no training or evaluation started "
    "in this cell. Keep CPU selected."
)

from pathlib import Path, PurePosixPath
from datetime import datetime
from zoneinfo import ZoneInfo
from importlib import metadata
from pip._vendor.packaging.requirements import Requirement
import hashlib, json, os, shutil, signal, stat
import subprocess, sys, tempfile, time, zipfile
import torch
from google.colab import drive

print("PyTorch:", torch.__version__, "CUDA:", torch.version.cuda, flush=True)
assert torch.cuda.is_available(), "Select A100 GPU, save, and reconnect first."
print("GPU:", torch.cuda.get_device_name(0), flush=True)
assert "A100" in torch.cuda.get_device_name(0)
assert torch.cuda.get_device_properties(0).total_memory / 2**30 >= 39
assert str(torch.__version__) == "2.11.0+cu130"
assert torch.version.cuda == "13.0"
drive.mount("/content/drive")

ROOT = Path("/content/ATML-Assignment-2")
PREP = Path(
    "/content/drive/MyDrive/ATML-Assignment-2/setup_evidence/"
    "20261003_161419_807248/"
    "standard_complete_evaluation_jobs_20261004_145929_395368"
)

def sha(path):
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    with Path(path).open("rb") as handle:
        for chunk in iter(lambda: handle.read(2**20), b""):
            digest.update(chunk)
    return digest.hexdigest()

for name, digest in json.loads(
    (PREP / "evidence_sha256.json").read_text()
).items():
    assert sha(PREP / name) == digest, f"Prepared evidence changed: {name}"

bundle = json.loads((PREP / "policy_evaluation_jobs.json").read_text())
assert bundle["status"] == "PREPARED_POLICY_EVALUATION_NOT_STARTED"
assert bundle["ML_settings_changed"] is False
assert [j["condition"] for j in bundle["jobs"]] == ["standard", "sft"]

SAVED = Path(bundle["project_backup"])
assert SAVED == Path(
    "/content/drive/MyDrive/ATML-Assignment-2/runtime_backups/"
    "20261004_142324_585826"
)
manifest = json.loads((SAVED / "backup_manifest.json").read_text())
assert manifest["status"] == "BACKUP_VERIFIED"
assert manifest["archive_root"] == ROOT.name
assert manifest["archive_sha256"] == bundle["project_archive_sha256"]
expected = manifest["file_sha256"]
assert len(expected) == 279
assert set(expected) == set(manifest["included_files"])
assert sha(SAVED / "project.zip") == manifest["archive_sha256"]

print("Restoring verified evaluation code/data...", flush=True)
if ROOT.exists():
    differences = [
        n for n, d in expected.items()
        if not (ROOT / n).is_file() or sha(ROOT / n) != d
    ]
    assert not differences, (
        f"Existing project differs; preserve it and paste: {differences[:10]}"
    )
else:
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(
        prefix="atml_eval_restore_", dir="/content"
    ) as temporary:
        with zipfile.ZipFile(SAVED / "project.zip") as archive:
            entries = archive.infolist()
            names = {
                str(PurePosixPath(ROOT.name) / name)
                for name in expected
            }
            assert len(entries) == len(names)
            assert {e.filename for e in entries} == names
            for entry in entries:
                path = PurePosixPath(entry.filename)
                assert not path.is_absolute()
                assert ".." not in path.parts
                assert path.parts[0] == ROOT.name
                assert not entry.is_dir()
                assert not stat.S_ISLNK(entry.external_attr >> 16)
            archive.extractall(temporary)
        restored = Path(temporary) / ROOT.name
        for name, digest in expected.items():
            assert sha(restored / name) == digest, f"Restore differs: {name}"
        shutil.move(str(restored), str(ROOT))

receipt_path = Path("/content/ATML_PA2_setup_receipt.json")
receipt_bytes = (SAVED / receipt_path.name).read_bytes()
assert not receipt_path.exists() or receipt_path.read_bytes() == receipt_bytes
receipt_path.write_bytes(receipt_bytes)
EVIDENCE = Path(json.loads(receipt_bytes)["backup_directory"])
assert EVIDENCE.is_dir()

BACKUP = Path(bundle["evaluation_backup_root"])
assert BACKUP == Path(
    "/content/drive/MyDrive/ATML-Assignment-2/evaluation_runs"
)
BACKUP.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

for job in bundle["jobs"]:
    name = job["condition"]
    assert job["phase"] == "policy"
    assert job["generation_pools"] == {
        "dpo_standard_eval": 300, "word_limit_prompts": 10
    }
    assert job["pair_pools"] == (
        {"dpo_standard_eval": 300, "dpo_length_eval": 246}
        if name == "standard" else {}
    )
    assert not Path(job["output"]).exists(), (
        "Evaluation output exists; preserve it and paste the output."
    )
    assert not [
        p for p in BACKUP.glob(f"eval_{name}_policy_*") if p.is_dir()
    ], "A saved evaluation attempt exists; paste the output before rerunning."

    args = [
        "-m", "task1_dpo.evaluate", "--config", "configs/dpo.yaml",
        "--name", name, "--phase", "policy",
        "--models-dir", bundle["local_models_dir"],
        "--output", job["output"], "--backup-root", str(BACKUP),
    ]
    if name == "standard":
        args += ["--adapter", bundle["local_adapter"]]
    assert job["command_arguments"] == args

stamp = datetime.now(ZoneInfo("Asia/Karachi")).strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S_%f")
STAGE = ROOT / "results/setup" / ("gpu_standard_sft_policy_" + stamp)
STAGE.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=False)

record = {
    "status": "PREPARING",
    "prepared_jobs": str(PREP),
    "bundle": bundle,
    "ML_settings_changed": False,
    "training_invoked": False,
    "evaluation_invoked": False,
    "completed_jobs": [],
    "gpu": torch.cuda.get_device_name(0),
    "torch": str(torch.__version__),
}
started = time.perf_counter()

try:
    shutil.copyfile(
        PREP / "policy_evaluation_jobs.json", STAGE / "prepared_jobs.json"
    )
    shutil.copyfile(
        PREP / "training_completion.json", STAGE / "training_completion.json"
    )

    # Restore package versions without replacing GPU PyTorch.
    pins = {
        "transformers": "4.57.1", "tokenizers": "0.22.1",
        "peft": "0.17.1", "trl": "0.27.2", "bitsandbytes": "0.50.2",
    }
    constraints = STAGE / "constraints.txt"
    constraints.write_text(
        "\n".join(f"{name}=={version}" for name, version in pins.items())
        + "\ntorch==2.11.0+cu130\nhuggingface_hub<1\n"
    )
    requirements = [
        Requirement(line.split("#", 1)[0].strip())
        for line in (ROOT / "requirements.txt").read_text().splitlines()
        if line.split("#", 1)[0].strip()
    ]
    requirements += [
        Requirement(f"{name}=={version}") for name, version in pins.items()
    ]
    requirements += [Requirement("huggingface_hub>=0.27,<1")]

    def mismatches():
        missing = []
        for requirement in requirements:
            if requirement.marker and not requirement.marker.evaluate():
                continue
            try:
                installed = metadata.version(requirement.name)
            except metadata.PackageNotFoundError:
                missing.append(str(requirement))
                continue
            if installed not in requirement.specifier:
                missing.append(f"{requirement}: found {installed}")
        return sorted(set(missing))

    problems = mismatches()
    record["package_restoration_needed"] = bool(problems)
    if problems:
        print("Restoring pinned packages:", flush=True)
        print("\n".join(problems), flush=True)
        install = subprocess.run(
            [
                sys.executable, "-m", "pip", "install",
                "-r", str(ROOT / "requirements.txt"),
                "-c", str(constraints),
            ],
            cwd=ROOT, text=True, capture_output=True,
        )
        (STAGE / "installation.log").write_text(
            install.stdout + "\n" + install.stderr
        )
        if install.returncode:
            print("\n".join(
                (install.stdout + install.stderr).splitlines()[-40:]
            ))
            raise RuntimeError("Package restoration failed; paste the output.")
    else:
        print("Pinned packages match; installation skipped.", flush=True)
        (STAGE / "installation.log").write_text(
            "Skipped: required versions already installed.\n"
        )

    assert not mismatches(), mismatches()
    assert metadata.version("torch") == "2.11.0+cu130"
    record["packages"] = {
        name: metadata.version(name) for name in pins
    }

    # Restore saved policy files. Reward weights remain on Drive.
    MODELS = Path(bundle["local_models_dir"])
    SOURCE_MODELS = Path(bundle["drive_models_dir"])
    manifest_path = SOURCE_MODELS / "model_manifest.json"
    assert sha(manifest_path) == bundle["model_manifest_sha256"]
    model_manifest = json.loads(manifest_path.read_text())
    policy = model_manifest["policy"]
    assert (policy["model_id"], policy["revision"]) == (
        "Qwen/Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct",
        "989aa7980e4cf806f80c7fef2b1adb7bc71aa306",
    )

    source = (SOURCE_MODELS / policy["directory"]).resolve()
    destination = (MODELS / policy["directory"]).resolve()
    assert SOURCE_MODELS.resolve() in source.parents
    assert MODELS.resolve() in destination.parents
    destination.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    print("Restoring saved policy files; no model download...", flush=True)
    for index, (name, digest) in enumerate(policy["sha256"].items(), 1):
        origin = (source / name).resolve()
        target = (destination / name).resolve()
        assert source in origin.parents and destination in target.parents
        if not target.exists():
            target.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            shutil.copyfile(origin, target)
        assert sha(target) == digest, f"Policy file differs: {name}"
        print(
            f"Policy file {index}/{len(policy['sha256'])}: {name}",
            flush=True,
        )

    local_manifest = MODELS / "model_manifest.json"
    if local_manifest.exists():
        assert sha(local_manifest) == bundle["model_manifest_sha256"]
    else:
        shutil.copyfile(manifest_path, local_manifest)
    assert sha(local_manifest) == bundle["model_manifest_sha256"]

    # Only these four checkpoint files are needed for policy evaluation.
    checkpoint = Path(bundle["completed_checkpoint"])
    assert checkpoint == Path(
        "/content/drive/MyDrive/ATML-Assignment-2/training_runs/"
        "standard_20261004_143635_293670_COMPLETE_RELOAD_VERIFIED"
    )
    adapter = Path(bundle["local_adapter"])
    assert adapter == Path(
        "/content/ATML_PA2_adapters/standard_completed_scale1024"
    )
    adapter.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    assert set(bundle["checkpoint_file_sha256"]) == {
        "run_record.json", "reload_verification.json",
        "adapter_config.json", "adapter_model.safetensors",
    }
    print("Restoring completed standard adapter; no training...", flush=True)
    for name, digest in bundle["checkpoint_file_sha256"].items():
        origin, target = checkpoint / name, adapter / name
        assert sha(origin) == digest, f"Saved checkpoint changed: {name}"
        if not target.exists():
            shutil.copyfile(origin, target)
        assert sha(target) == digest, f"Local checkpoint differs: {name}"

    trained = json.loads((adapter / "run_record.json").read_text())
    assert trained["status"] == "COMPLETE_RELOAD_VERIFIED"
    assert (trained["completed_pairs"], trained["completed_updates"]) == (
        1500, 94
    )

    environment = dict(
        os.environ, PYTHONUNBUFFERED="1", HF_HUB_OFFLINE="1"
    )
    for job in bundle["jobs"]:
        name = job["condition"]
        record.update({
            "status": "EVALUATION_RUNNING",
            "evaluation_invoked": True,
            "current_condition": name,
        })
        (STAGE / "launch_record.json").write_text(
            json.dumps(record, indent=2)
        )
        print(
            f"STARTING {name.upper()} POLICY EVALUATION: "
            f"pairs={job['pair_pools']}; "
            f"generation={job['generation_pools']}",
            flush=True,
        )
        with (STAGE / f"{name}_console.log").open(
            "w", encoding="utf-8"
        ) as log:
            process = subprocess.Popen(
                [sys.executable, "-u", *job["command_arguments"]],
                cwd=ROOT, env=environment,
                stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
                text=True, bufsize=1, start_new_session=True,
            )
            try:
                for line in process.stdout:
                    print(line, end="", flush=True)
                    log.write(line)
                    log.flush()
                code = process.wait()
            except BaseException:
                if process.poll() is None:
                    os.killpg(process.pid, signal.SIGINT)
                    process.wait()
                raise

        assert code == 0, (
            f"{name} evaluation failed; paste the output. "
            "Do not rerun automatically."
        )
        output = Path(job["output"])
        result = json.loads((output / "evaluation_record.json").read_text())
        assert result["status"] == "POLICY_COMPLETE"
        assert result["phase"] == "policy"
        assert result["plan"]["name"] == name

        metrics = json.loads((output / "metrics.json").read_text())
        assert set(metrics["pairs"]) == set(job["pair_pools"])
        assert set(metrics["generation"]) == set(job["generation_pools"])
        for key, count in job["pair_pools"].items():
            assert metrics["pairs"][key]["pairs"] == count
        for key, count in job["generation_pools"].items():
            assert metrics["generation"][key]["responses"] == count
        if name == "sft":
            assert result["adapter"] is None

        backup_name = (
            f"eval_{name}_policy_{result['started_at']}_POLICY_COMPLETE"
        )
        receipt = json.loads(
            (BACKUP / (backup_name + "_backup_receipt.json")).read_text()
        )
        assert receipt["status"] == "BACKUP_VERIFIED"
        saved = Path(receipt["destination"])
        assert saved == BACKUP / backup_name
        for filename in ("evaluation_record.json", "metrics.json"):
            assert (
                sha(saved / filename)
                == sha(output / filename)
                == receipt["sha256"][filename]
            )

        record["completed_jobs"].append({
            "condition": name,
            "phase": "policy",
            "status": "POLICY_COMPLETE",
            "verified_backup": str(saved),
            "evaluation_record_sha256": sha(
                saved / "evaluation_record.json"
            ),
            "metrics_sha256": sha(saved / "metrics.json"),
        })
        (STAGE / "launch_record.json").write_text(
            json.dumps(record, indent=2)
        )
        concise = {
            "condition": name,
            "pairs": metrics["pairs"],
            "generation": {
                key: {
                    k: v for k, v in values.items()
                    if k not in {
                        "inherited_generation_config",
                        "effective_generation_config",
                    }
                }
                for key, values in metrics["generation"].items()
            },
        }
        print(json.dumps(concise, indent=2), flush=True)
        print(
            f"{name.upper()} POLICY RESULTS VERIFIED:", saved, flush=True
        )

    record["status"] = "STANDARD_AND_SFT_POLICY_EVALUATION_COMPLETE"

except BaseException as error:
    record["status"] = "FAILED"
    record["error"] = f"{type(error).__name__}: {error}"
    raise

finally:
    record["launcher_wall_seconds"] = time.perf_counter() - started
    (STAGE / "launch_record.json").write_text(
        json.dumps(record, indent=2)
    )
    copied = EVIDENCE / STAGE.name
    hashes = {
        str(p.relative_to(STAGE)): sha(p)
        for p in STAGE.rglob("*") if p.is_file()
    }
    shutil.copytree(STAGE, copied)
    assert hashes == {
        str(p.relative_to(copied)): sha(p)
        for p in copied.rglob("*") if p.is_file()
    }, "Launcher backup failed; keep runtime connected."

    print("Launch evidence saved and verified:", copied, flush=True)
    print("Flushing Drive writes before GPU release...", flush=True)
    drive.flush_and_unmount(timeout_ms=120000)
    print("DRIVE_WRITES_FLUSHED", flush=True)

print("STANDARD_AND_SFT_POLICY_EVALUATION_COMPLETE")
for result in record["completed_jobs"]:
    print(result["condition"], "saved:", result["verified_backup"])
print(
    "Reward scoring is pending. Save notebook, disconnect/delete GPU, "
    "then paste output."
)

# CPU only: verify saved evaluations and prepare reward scoring.
import hashlib
import json
from datetime import datetime
from pathlib import Path
from zoneinfo import ZoneInfo
import torch
from google.colab import drive

assert not torch.cuda.is_available(), "Select CPU before running this block."
drive.mount("/content/drive")

BASE = Path("/content/drive/MyDrive/ATML-Assignment-2")
EVIDENCE = BASE / "setup_evidence/20261003_161419_807248"
PREP = EVIDENCE / "standard_complete_evaluation_jobs_20261004_145929_395368"
LAUNCH = EVIDENCE / "gpu_standard_sft_policy_20261004_151459_318156"


def sha(path):
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    with Path(path).open("rb") as handle:
        for chunk in iter(lambda: handle.read(2**20), b""):
            digest.update(chunk)
    return digest.hexdigest()


def save_json(path, value):
    payload = json.dumps(value, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False, allow_nan=False)
    path.write_text(payload, encoding="utf-8")
    assert path.read_text(encoding="utf-8") == payload


for name, digest in json.loads((PREP / "evidence_sha256.json").read_text()).items():
    assert sha(PREP / name) == digest, f"Prepared evidence changed: {name}"

previous = json.loads((PREP / "policy_evaluation_jobs.json").read_text())
launch = json.loads((LAUNCH / "launch_record.json").read_text())
assert launch["status"] == "STANDARD_AND_SFT_POLICY_EVALUATION_COMPLETE"
assert launch["bundle"] == previous and launch["training_invoked"] is False
assert [j["condition"] for j in launch["completed_jobs"]] == ["standard", "sft"]

stamp = datetime.now(ZoneInfo("Asia/Karachi")).strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S_%f")
jobs, summaries = [], {}

for completed in launch["completed_jobs"]:
    name = completed["condition"]
    folder = Path(completed["verified_backup"])
    assert folder.parent == BASE / "evaluation_runs"

    receipt_path = folder.with_name(folder.name + "_backup_receipt.json")
    receipt = json.loads(receipt_path.read_text())
    assert receipt["status"] == "BACKUP_VERIFIED"
    assert Path(receipt["destination"]) == folder

    actual_files = {
        str(p.relative_to(folder)) for p in folder.rglob("*") if p.is_file()
    }
    assert actual_files == set(receipt["sha256"]), name
    print(f"Verifying saved {name} evaluation files...", flush=True)

    for relative, digest in receipt["sha256"].items():
        path = (folder / relative).resolve()
        assert folder.resolve() in path.parents
        assert sha(path) == digest, f"Saved file changed: {relative}"

    for filename, key in (
        ("evaluation_record.json", "evaluation_record_sha256"),
        ("metrics.json", "metrics_sha256"),
    ):
        assert receipt["sha256"][filename] == completed[key]

    record = json.loads((folder / "evaluation_record.json").read_text())
    metrics = json.loads((folder / "metrics.json").read_text())
    assert record["status"] == "POLICY_COMPLETE" and record["phase"] == "policy"
    assert record["plan"]["name"] == metrics["name"] == name
    assert record["policy_parameters_unchanged"] is True
    assert all(
        receipt["sha256"][r] == d
        for r, d in record["artifact_sha256"].items()
    )

    if name == "sft":
        assert record["adapter"] is None
    else:
        assert record["checkpoint_file_sha256"] == previous["checkpoint_file_sha256"]

    expected_pairs = (
        {"dpo_standard_eval": 300, "dpo_length_eval": 246}
        if name == "standard" else {}
    )
    assert set(metrics["pairs"]) == set(expected_pairs)
    assert all(
        metrics["pairs"][key]["pairs"] == count
        for key, count in expected_pairs.items()
    )

    generations = {"dpo_standard_eval": 300, "word_limit_prompts": 10}
    assert set(metrics["generation"]) == set(generations)

    for key, count in generations.items():
        rows = [
            json.loads(line)
            for line in (folder / f"generation_{key}.jsonl").read_text().splitlines()
        ]
        assert len(rows) == metrics["generation"][key]["responses"] == count
        assert [r["row_index"] for r in rows] == list(range(count))
        assert [r["prompt_id"] for r in rows] == (
            record["plan"]["datasets"][key]["ordered_prompt_ids"]
        )

    summaries[name] = {
        "pairs": metrics["pairs"],
        "generation": {
            key: {
                k: v for k, v in values.items()
                if k not in {
                    "inherited_generation_config", "effective_generation_config"
                }
            }
            for key, values in metrics["generation"].items()
        },
    }

    local_results = f"/content/ATML_PA2_policy_results/{name}_completed"
    output = f"/content/ATML-Assignment-2/results/task1_dpo/{name}/reward_{stamp}"
    jobs.append({
        "condition": name,
        "phase": "reward",
        "output": output,
        "policy_results_drive": str(folder),
        "policy_results_local": local_results,
        "policy_results_sha256": receipt["sha256"],
        "generation_pools": generations,
        "command_arguments": [
            "-m", "task1_dpo.evaluate", "--config", "configs/dpo.yaml",
            "--name", name, "--phase", "reward",
            "--models-dir", previous["local_models_dir"],
            "--policy-results", local_results,
            "--output", output,
            "--backup-root", previous["evaluation_backup_root"],
        ],
    })

bundle = {
    key: previous[key] for key in (
        "project_backup", "project_archive_sha256",
        "drive_models_dir", "local_models_dir",
        "model_manifest_sha256", "evaluation_backup_root",
    )
}
bundle.update({
    "status": "PREPARED_REWARD_SCORING_NOT_STARTED",
    "jobs": jobs,
    "ML_settings_changed": False,
    "new_answers_generated": False,
    "reward_configuration_fix":
        "Previously approved in-memory rope_theta=1000000 compatibility correction.",
    "next_job_checks":
        "Existing evaluator verifies all complete reward inputs fit 4096 before "
        "loading reward weights; approved loader checks GPU placement and rotary settings.",
    "policy_launch_evidence": str(LAUNCH),
    "policy_launch_record_sha256": sha(LAUNCH / "launch_record.json"),
})

STAGE = EVIDENCE / ("policy_complete_reward_jobs_" + stamp)
STAGE.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=False)
save_json(STAGE / "policy_results_summary.json", summaries)
save_json(STAGE / "reward_evaluation_jobs.json", bundle)
save_json(STAGE / "evidence_sha256.json", {
    name: sha(STAGE / name)
    for name in ("policy_results_summary.json", "reward_evaluation_jobs.json")
})

print("POLICY_RESULTS_AND_REWARD_JOBS_OK")
print("Verified: 546 preference pairs and 620 saved answers across standard/SFT.")
print("Prepared reward scoring: 310 standard answers + 310 baseline answers.")
print("Evidence saved and verified:", STAGE)
print("Keep CPU selected. No weights loaded, answers regenerated, or settings changed.")

from pathlib import Path, PurePosixPath

from zoneinfo import ZoneInfo

print("PyTorch:", torch.__version__, "CUDA:", torch.version.cuda, flush=True)
assert torch.cuda.is_available(), "Select A100 GPU, save, and reconnect first."
print("GPU:", torch.cuda.get_device_name(0), flush=True)
assert "A100" in torch.cuda.get_device_name(0)
assert torch.cuda.get_device_properties(0).total_memory / 2**30 >= 39
assert str(torch.__version__) == "2.11.0+cu130" and torch.version.cuda == "13.0", (
    "GPU environment differs; paste the output."
)

def sha(path):
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    with Path(path).open("rb") as handle:
        for chunk in iter(lambda: handle.read(2**20), b""):
            digest.update(chunk)
    return digest.hexdigest()


for name, digest in json.loads((PREP / "evidence_sha256.json").read_text()).items():
    assert sha(PREP / name) == digest, f"Prepared evidence changed: {name}"

bundle = json.loads((PREP / "reward_evaluation_jobs.json").read_text())

SAVED = Path(bundle["project_backup"])

    "/content/drive/MyDrive/ATML-Assignment-2/"
    "runtime_backups/20261004_142324_585826"

expected = manifest["file_sha256"]

assert set(expected) == set(manifest["included_files"])
assert sha(SAVED / "project.zip") == manifest["archive_sha256"]

print("Restoring verified evaluation code/data...", flush=True)
if ROOT.exists():
    differences = [
        n for n, d in expected.items()
        if not (ROOT / n).is_file() or sha(ROOT / n) != d
    ]
    assert not differences, (
        f"Existing project differs; preserve it and paste: {differences[:10]}"
    )
else:
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(
        prefix="atml_eval_restore_", dir="/content"
    ) as temporary:
        with zipfile.ZipFile(SAVED / "project.zip") as archive:
            entries = archive.infolist()
            names = {
                str(PurePosixPath(ROOT.name) / name) for name in expected
            }
            assert len(entries) == len(names)
            assert {e.filename for e in entries} == names
            for entry in entries:
                path = PurePosixPath(entry.filename)
                assert not path.is_absolute() and ".." not in path.parts
                assert path.parts[0] == ROOT.name
                assert not entry.is_dir()
                assert not stat.S_ISLNK(entry.external_attr >> 16)
            archive.extractall(temporary)
        restored = Path(temporary) / ROOT.name
        for name, digest in expected.items():
            assert sha(restored / name) == digest, name
        shutil.move(str(restored), str(ROOT))

receipt_path = Path("/content/ATML_PA2_setup_receipt.json")
receipt_bytes = (SAVED / receipt_path.name).read_bytes()
assert not receipt_path.exists() or receipt_path.read_bytes() == receipt_bytes
receipt_path.write_bytes(receipt_bytes)
EVIDENCE = Path(json.loads(receipt_bytes)["backup_directory"])
assert EVIDENCE.is_dir()
