assert BACKUP == Path(
    "/content/drive/MyDrive/ATML-Assignment-2/evaluation_runs"
)
BACKUP.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

for job in bundle["jobs"]:
    name = job["condition"]
    assert job["phase"] == "reward"
    assert job["generation_pools"] == {
        "dpo_standard_eval": 300, "word_limit_prompts": 10
    }
    assert not Path(job["output"]).exists(), (
        "Reward output exists; preserve it and paste the output."
    )
    assert not [
        p for p in BACKUP.glob(f"eval_{name}_reward_*") if p.is_dir()
    ], "A reward attempt exists; paste the output before rerunning."

    args = [
        "-m", "task1_dpo.evaluate", "--config", "configs/dpo.yaml",
        "--name", name, "--phase", "reward",
        "--models-dir", bundle["local_models_dir"],
        "--policy-results", job["policy_results_local"],
        "--output", job["output"], "--backup-root", str(BACKUP),
    ]
    assert job["command_arguments"] == args

stamp = datetime.now(ZoneInfo("Asia/Karachi")).strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S_%f")
STAGE = ROOT / "results/setup" / ("gpu_standard_sft_reward_" + stamp)
STAGE.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=False)
record = {
    "status": "PREPARING", "prepared_jobs": str(PREP), "bundle": bundle,
    "ML_settings_changed": False, "training_invoked": False,
    "evaluation_invoked": False, "new_answers_generated": False,
    "completed_jobs": [], "gpu": torch.cuda.get_device_name(0),
    "torch": str(torch.__version__),
}
started = time.perf_counter()

try:
    shutil.copyfile(
        PREP / "reward_evaluation_jobs.json", STAGE / "prepared_jobs.json"
    )
    shutil.copyfile(
        PREP / "policy_results_summary.json",
        STAGE / "policy_results_summary.json"
    )

    # Restore mismatched packages while preserving GPU PyTorch.
    pins = {
        "transformers": "4.57.1", "tokenizers": "0.22.1",
        "peft": "0.17.1", "trl": "0.27.2", "bitsandbytes": "0.50.2",
    }
    constraints = STAGE / "constraints.txt"
    constraints.write_text(
        "\n".join(f"{name}=={version}" for name, version in pins.items())
        + "\ntorch==2.11.0+cu130\nhuggingface_hub<1\n"
    )
    requirements = [
        Requirement(line.split("#", 1)[0].strip())
        for line in (ROOT / "requirements.txt").read_text().splitlines()
        if line.split("#", 1)[0].strip()
    ]
    requirements += [
        Requirement(f"{name}=={version}") for name, version in pins.items()
    ]
    requirements += [Requirement("huggingface_hub>=0.27,<1")]

    def mismatches():
        missing = []
        for requirement in requirements:
            if requirement.marker and not requirement.marker.evaluate():
                continue
            try:
                installed = metadata.version(requirement.name)
            except metadata.PackageNotFoundError:
                missing.append(str(requirement))
                continue
            if installed not in requirement.specifier:
                missing.append(f"{requirement}: found {installed}")
        return sorted(set(missing))

    problems = mismatches()
    record["package_restoration_needed"] = bool(problems)
    if problems:
        print("Restoring pinned packages:", flush=True)
        print("\n".join(problems), flush=True)
        install = subprocess.run(
            [
                sys.executable, "-m", "pip", "install",
                "-r", str(ROOT / "requirements.txt"),
                "-c", str(constraints),
            ],
            cwd=ROOT, text=True, capture_output=True,
        )
        (STAGE / "installation.log").write_text(
            install.stdout + "\n" + install.stderr
        )
        if install.returncode:
            print("\n".join(
                (install.stdout + install.stderr).splitlines()[-40:]
            ))
            raise RuntimeError("Package restoration failed; paste the output.")
    else:
        print("Pinned packages match; installation skipped.", flush=True)
        (STAGE / "installation.log").write_text(
            "Skipped: required versions already installed.\n"
        )
    assert not mismatches(), mismatches()
    assert metadata.version("torch") == "2.11.0+cu130"
    record["packages"] = {
        name: metadata.version(name) for name in pins
    }

    # Existing loader verifies both snapshots, but loads only reward weights.
    MODELS = Path(bundle["local_models_dir"])
    SOURCE_MODELS = Path(bundle["drive_models_dir"])
    manifest_path = SOURCE_MODELS / "model_manifest.json"
    assert sha(manifest_path) == bundle["model_manifest_sha256"]
    model_manifest = json.loads(manifest_path.read_text())
    identities = {
        "policy": (
            "Qwen/Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct",
            "989aa7980e4cf806f80c7fef2b1adb7bc71aa306",
        ),
        "reward": (
            "yavuz-ai/qwen2.5-1.5b-rm-ultrafeedback",
            "f98759a4a1ccdc47a3136748b6bfea9a32ba8fcc",
        ),
    }
    for kind, identity in identities.items():
        entry = model_manifest[kind]
        assert (entry["model_id"], entry["revision"]) == identity
        source = (SOURCE_MODELS / entry["directory"]).resolve()
        destination = (MODELS / entry["directory"]).resolve()
        assert SOURCE_MODELS.resolve() in source.parents
        assert MODELS.resolve() in destination.parents
        destination.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        print(f"Restoring saved {kind} files; no model download...", flush=True)
        for index, (name, digest) in enumerate(entry["sha256"].items(), 1):
            origin = (source / name).resolve()
            target = (destination / name).resolve()
            assert source in origin.parents and destination in target.parents
            if not target.exists():
                target.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
                shutil.copyfile(origin, target)
            assert sha(target) == digest, f"Prepared {kind} file differs: {name}"
            print(
                f"{kind} file {index}/{len(entry['sha256'])}: {name}",
                flush=True,
            )
    local_manifest = MODELS / "model_manifest.json"
    if not local_manifest.exists():
        shutil.copyfile(manifest_path, local_manifest)
    assert sha(local_manifest) == bundle["model_manifest_sha256"]

    # Restore saved answers and metadata for replay.
    for job in bundle["jobs"]:
        source = Path(job["policy_results_drive"]).resolve()
        destination = Path(job["policy_results_local"]).resolve()
        assert source.parent == BACKUP.resolve()
        assert destination.parent == Path("/content/ATML_PA2_policy_results")
        if not destination.exists():
            shutil.copytree(source, destination)
        expected_results = job["policy_results_sha256"]
        assert set(expected_results) == {
            str(p.relative_to(destination))
            for p in destination.rglob("*") if p.is_file()
        }
        for relative, digest in expected_results.items():
            path = (destination / relative).resolve()
            assert destination in path.parents and sha(path) == digest, relative
        print(
            f"Saved {job['condition']} answers restored and verified.",
            flush=True,
        )

    environment = dict(
        os.environ, PYTHONUNBUFFERED="1", HF_HUB_OFFLINE="1"
    )
    for job in bundle["jobs"]:
        name = job["condition"]
        record.update({
            "status": "EVALUATION_RUNNING",
            "evaluation_invoked": True, "current_condition": name,
        })
        (STAGE / "launch_record.json").write_text(
            json.dumps(record, indent=2)
        )
        print(
            f"STARTING {name.upper()} REWARD SCORING: "
            f"{sum(job['generation_pools'].values())} saved answers.",
            flush=True,
        )
        with (STAGE / f"{name}_console.log").open(
            "w", encoding="utf-8"
        ) as log:
            process = subprocess.Popen(
                [sys.executable, "-u", *job["command_arguments"]],
                cwd=ROOT, env=environment,
                stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
                text=True, bufsize=1, start_new_session=True,
            )
            try:
                for line in process.stdout:
                    print(line, end="", flush=True)
                    log.write(line)
                    log.flush()
                code = process.wait()
            except BaseException:
                if process.poll() is None:
                    os.killpg(process.pid, signal.SIGINT)
                    process.wait()
                raise
        assert code == 0, (
            f"{name} scoring failed; paste the output. Do not rerun automatically."
        )

        output = Path(job["output"])
        result = json.loads((output / "evaluation_record.json").read_text())
        assert result["status"] == "COMPLETE" and result["phase"] == "reward"
        assert result["plan"]["name"] == name
        assert result["complete_reward_inputs_verified"] is True

        metrics = json.loads((output / "metrics.json").read_text())
        audit = json.loads((output / "reward_input_audit.json").read_text())
        loading = json.loads((output / "reward_loading.json").read_text())
        assert loading["declared_theta"] == 1000000.0
        assert loading["original_file_unchanged"]
        assert loading["loaded_position_frequencies_match"]
        assert set(metrics["generation"]) == set(job["generation_pools"])

        concise = {"condition": name, "reward_scores": {}}
        for key, count in job["generation_pools"].items():
            values = metrics["generation"][key]
            assert values["responses"] == values["reward_score"]["count"] == count
            assert audit[key]["responses"] == count
            assert audit[key]["truncated"] == 0
            assert audit[key]["maximum_complete_input_tokens"] <= 4096
            concise["reward_scores"][key] = values["reward_score"]

        backup_name = f"eval_{name}_reward_{result['started_at']}_COMPLETE"
        receipt = json.loads(
            (BACKUP / (backup_name + "_backup_receipt.json")).read_text()
        )
        assert receipt["status"] == "BACKUP_VERIFIED"
        saved = Path(receipt["destination"])
        assert saved == BACKUP / backup_name
        for filename in (
            "evaluation_record.json", "metrics.json",
            "reward_input_audit.json", "reward_loading.json",
        ):
            assert (
                sha(saved / filename) == sha(output / filename)
                == receipt["sha256"][filename]
            )
        record["completed_jobs"].append({
            "condition": name, "phase": "reward", "status": "COMPLETE",
            "verified_backup": str(saved),
            "evaluation_record_sha256": sha(saved / "evaluation_record.json"),
            "metrics_sha256": sha(saved / "metrics.json"),
        })
        (STAGE / "launch_record.json").write_text(
            json.dumps(record, indent=2)
        )
        print(json.dumps(concise, indent=2), flush=True)
        print(f"{name.upper()} REWARD RESULTS VERIFIED:", saved, flush=True)

    record["status"] = "STANDARD_AND_SFT_REWARD_SCORING_COMPLETE"

except BaseException as error:
    record["status"] = "FAILED"
    record["error"] = f"{type(error).__name__}: {error}"
    raise

finally:
    record["launcher_wall_seconds"] = time.perf_counter() - started
    (STAGE / "launch_record.json").write_text(json.dumps(record, indent=2))
    copied = EVIDENCE / STAGE.name
    hashes = {
        str(p.relative_to(STAGE)): sha(p)
        for p in STAGE.rglob("*") if p.is_file()
    }
    shutil.copytree(STAGE, copied)
    assert hashes == {
        str(p.relative_to(copied)): sha(p)
        for p in copied.rglob("*") if p.is_file()
    }, "Launcher backup failed; keep runtime connected."
    print("Launch evidence saved and verified:", copied, flush=True)
    print("Flushing Drive writes before GPU release...", flush=True)
    drive.flush_and_unmount(timeout_ms=120000)
    print("DRIVE_WRITES_FLUSHED", flush=True)

print("STANDARD_AND_SFT_REWARD_SCORING_COMPLETE")
for result in record["completed_jobs"]:
    print(result["condition"], "saved:", result["verified_backup"])
print("Save notebook; disconnect/delete GPU; paste the complete output.")

# CPU only: record rewards and prepare the four approved training runs.
import hashlib
import json
import zipfile
from datetime import datetime
from pathlib import Path
from zoneinfo import ZoneInfo
import torch
from google.colab import drive

assert not torch.cuda.is_available(), "Select CPU before running this block."
drive.mount("/content/drive")

BASE = Path("/content/drive/MyDrive/ATML-Assignment-2")
EVIDENCE = BASE / "setup_evidence/20261003_161419_807248"
PREP = EVIDENCE / "policy_complete_reward_jobs_20261004_164606_103611"
LAUNCH = EVIDENCE / "gpu_standard_sft_reward_20261004_165852_548773"


def sha(path):
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    with Path(path).open("rb") as handle:
        for chunk in iter(lambda: handle.read(2**20), b""):
            digest.update(chunk)
    return digest.hexdigest()


def save_json(path, value):
    payload = json.dumps(value, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False, allow_nan=False)
    path.write_text(payload, encoding="utf-8")
    assert path.read_text(encoding="utf-8") == payload


for name, digest in json.loads((PREP / "evidence_sha256.json").read_text()).items():
    assert sha(PREP / name) == digest, name

previous = json.loads((PREP / "reward_evaluation_jobs.json").read_text())
launch = json.loads((LAUNCH / "launch_record.json").read_text())
assert launch["status"] == "STANDARD_AND_SFT_REWARD_SCORING_COMPLETE"
assert launch["bundle"] == previous and launch["training_invoked"] is False
assert launch["new_answers_generated"] is False
assert [j["condition"] for j in launch["completed_jobs"]] == ["standard", "sft"]

results, saved_results, configuration = {}, {}, None
for completed in launch["completed_jobs"]:
    name = completed["condition"]
    folder = Path(completed["verified_backup"])
    assert folder.parent == BASE / "evaluation_runs"

    receipt = json.loads(
        folder.with_name(folder.name + "_backup_receipt.json").read_text()
    )
    assert receipt["status"] == "BACKUP_VERIFIED"
    assert Path(receipt["destination"]) == folder
    assert set(receipt["sha256"]) == {
        str(p.relative_to(folder)) for p in folder.rglob("*") if p.is_file()
    }
    for relative, digest in receipt["sha256"].items():
        path = (folder / relative).resolve()
        assert folder.resolve() in path.parents
        assert sha(path) == digest, relative

    record = json.loads((folder / "evaluation_record.json").read_text())
    metrics = json.loads((folder / "metrics.json").read_text())
    audit = json.loads((folder / "reward_input_audit.json").read_text())
    loading = json.loads((folder / "reward_loading.json").read_text())

    assert record["status"] == "COMPLETE" and record["plan"]["name"] == name
    assert record["phase"] == "reward" and record["complete_reward_inputs_verified"]
    assert sha(folder / "evaluation_record.json") == completed["evaluation_record_sha256"]
    assert sha(folder / "metrics.json") == completed["metrics_sha256"]
    assert loading["original_file_unchanged"]
    assert loading["loaded_position_frequencies_match"]
    assert loading["declared_theta"] == 1000000.0

    for key, count in {
        "dpo_standard_eval": 300, "word_limit_prompts": 10
    }.items():
        assert metrics["generation"][key]["reward_score"]["count"] == count
        assert audit[key]["responses"] == count and audit[key]["truncated"] == 0
        assert audit[key]["maximum_complete_input_tokens"] <= 4096

    if configuration is None:
        configuration = record["effective_config"]
    assert configuration == record["effective_config"]

    results[name] = {
        "pairs": metrics["pairs"],
        "generation": {
            key: {
                k: v for k, v in values.items()
                if k not in {
                    "inherited_generation_config", "effective_generation_config"
                }
            }
            for key, values in metrics["generation"].items()
        },
    }
    saved_results[name] = {
        "folder": str(folder), "sha256": receipt["sha256"]
    }
    print(f"{name}: completed reward scoring and saved artifacts verified.", flush=True)

PROJECT = Path(previous["project_backup"])
manifest = json.loads((PROJECT / "backup_manifest.json").read_text())
assert manifest["status"] == "BACKUP_VERIFIED"
assert (
    sha(PROJECT / "project.zip")
    == manifest["archive_sha256"]
    == previous["project_archive_sha256"]
)

standard = BASE / "training_runs/standard_20261004_143635_293670_COMPLETE_RELOAD_VERIFIED"
standard_receipt = json.loads(
    standard.with_name(standard.name + "_backup_receipt.json").read_text()
)
assert standard_receipt["status"] == "BACKUP_VERIFIED"
assert sha(standard / "run_record.json") == standard_receipt["sha256"]["run_record.json"]
trained = json.loads((standard / "run_record.json").read_text())
assert trained["status"] == "COMPLETE_RELOAD_VERIFIED"
assert trained["effective_config"] == configuration
assert trained["scaler_settings"]["init_scale"] == 1024.0

stamp = datetime.now(ZoneInfo("Asia/Karachi")).strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S_%f")
jobs = []

with zipfile.ZipFile(PROJECT / "project.zip") as archive:
    for name, key, count, beta in (
        ("beta_003", "dpo_standard_train", 600, .03),
        ("beta_010", "dpo_standard_train", 600, .10),
        ("beta_030", "dpo_standard_train", 600, .30),
        ("length_balanced", "dpo_length_train", 1500, .10),
    ):
        relative = configuration["paths"][key]
        raw = archive.read(str(Path(manifest["archive_root"]) / relative))
        digest = hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest()
        assert digest == manifest["file_sha256"][relative]

        rows = [json.loads(line) for line in raw.decode("utf-8").splitlines()]
        assert len(rows) == 1500
        ids = [row["prompt_id"] for row in rows[:count]]
        if count == 600:
            assert ids == trained["selected_prompt_ids"][:600]

        windows = [
            min(16, count - offset) for offset in range(0, count, 16)
        ]
        output = f"/content/ATML-Assignment-2/outputs/task1_dpo/{name}_{stamp}"
        jobs.append({
            "condition": name,
            "path_key": key,
            "pairs": count,
            "beta": beta,
            "epochs": 1,
            "initial_gradient_scale": 1024.0,
            "optimizer_updates": len(windows),
            "update_pair_counts": windows,
            "selected_prompt_ids": ids,
            "dataset_sha256": digest,
            "output": output,
            "command_arguments": [
                "-m", "task1_dpo.train", "--config", "configs/dpo.yaml",
                "--run-name", name,
                "--models-dir", previous["local_models_dir"],
                "--output", output,
                "--backup-root", str(BASE / "training_runs"),
            ],
        })
        print(
            f"Prepared {name}: pairs={count}; beta={beta}; "
            f"updates={len(windows)}; tail={windows[-1]}."
        )

bundle = {
    key: previous[key] for key in (
        "project_backup", "project_archive_sha256",
        "drive_models_dir", "local_models_dir", "model_manifest_sha256",
    )
}
bundle.update({
    "status": "PREPARED_FOUR_COMPARISON_TRAINING_RUNS_NOT_STARTED",
    "jobs": jobs,
    "training_backup_root": str(BASE / "training_runs"),
    "ML_settings_changed": False,
    "initialization":
        "Each run uses the original pinned base, fresh LoRA adapters, optimizer, "
        "scaler and seed6304; no trained adapter reused.",
    "scaler_settings": trained["scaler_settings"],
    "effective_config": configuration,
    "standard_reference_record_sha256": sha(standard / "run_record.json"),
    "completed_standard_and_sft_results": saved_results,
})

STAGE = EVIDENCE / ("reward_complete_comparison_jobs_" + stamp)
STAGE.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=False)
save_json(STAGE / "standard_sft_complete_results.json", results)
save_json(STAGE / "comparison_training_jobs.json", bundle)
save_json(STAGE / "evidence_sha256.json", {
    name: sha(STAGE / name)
    for name in (
        "standard_sft_complete_results.json", "comparison_training_jobs.json"
    )
})

print("REWARD_RESULTS_AND_COMPARISON_JOBS_OK")
print("All 620 rewards verified; four independent full-budget training jobs prepared.")
print("Evidence saved and verified:", STAGE)
print("Keep CPU selected. No weights loaded, settings changed, or training started.")

from pathlib import Path, PurePosixPath
from datetime import datetime
from zoneinfo import ZoneInfo
from importlib import metadata
from pip._vendor.packaging.requirements import Requirement
import hashlib, json, os, shutil, signal, stat
import subprocess, sys, tempfile, time, zipfile
import torch
from google.colab import drive

print("PyTorch:", torch.__version__, "CUDA:", torch.version.cuda, flush=True)
assert torch.cuda.is_available(), "Select A100 GPU, save, and reconnect first."
print("GPU:", torch.cuda.get_device_name(0), flush=True)
assert "A100" in torch.cuda.get_device_name(0)
assert torch.cuda.get_device_properties(0).total_memory / 2**30 >= 39
assert str(torch.__version__) == "2.11.0+cu130" and torch.version.cuda == "13.0", (
    "GPU environment differs; paste the output."
)
drive.mount("/content/drive")

ROOT = Path("/content/ATML-Assignment-2")
PREP = Path(
    "/content/drive/MyDrive/ATML-Assignment-2/"
    "setup_evidence/20261003_161419_807248/"
    "reward_complete_comparison_jobs_20261004_171524_857140"
)


def sha(path):
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    with Path(path).open("rb") as handle:
        for chunk in iter(lambda: handle.read(2**20), b""):
            digest.update(chunk)
    return digest.hexdigest()


for name, digest in json.loads((PREP / "evidence_sha256.json").read_text()).items():
    assert sha(PREP / name) == digest, f"Prepared evidence changed: {name}"

bundle = json.loads((PREP / "comparison_training_jobs.json").read_text())
assert bundle["status"] == "PREPARED_FOUR_COMPARISON_TRAINING_RUNS_NOT_STARTED"
assert bundle["ML_settings_changed"] is False
assert [j["condition"] for j in bundle["jobs"]] == [
    "beta_003", "beta_010", "beta_030", "length_balanced"
]

SAVED = Path(bundle["project_backup"])
assert SAVED == Path(
    "/content/drive/MyDrive/ATML-Assignment-2/"
    "runtime_backups/20261004_142324_585826"
)
manifest = json.loads((SAVED / "backup_manifest.json").read_text())
assert manifest["status"] == "BACKUP_VERIFIED"
assert manifest["archive_root"] == ROOT.name
assert manifest["archive_sha256"] == bundle["project_archive_sha256"]
expected = manifest["file_sha256"]
assert len(expected) == 279
assert set(expected) == set(manifest["included_files"])
assert sha(SAVED / "project.zip") == manifest["archive_sha256"]

print("Restoring verified training code/data...", flush=True)
if ROOT.exists():
    differences = [
        n for n, d in expected.items()
        if not (ROOT / n).is_file() or sha(ROOT / n) != d
    ]
    assert not differences, (
        f"Existing project differs; preserve it and paste: {differences[:10]}"
    )
else:
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(
        prefix="atml_training_restore_", dir="/content"
    ) as temporary:
        with zipfile.ZipFile(SAVED / "project.zip") as archive:
            entries = archive.infolist()
            names = {
                str(PurePosixPath(ROOT.name) / name) for name in expected
            }
            assert len(entries) == len(names)
            assert {e.filename for e in entries} == names
            for entry in entries:
                path = PurePosixPath(entry.filename)
                assert not path.is_absolute() and ".." not in path.parts
                assert path.parts[0] == ROOT.name and not entry.is_dir()
                assert not stat.S_ISLNK(entry.external_attr >> 16)
            archive.extractall(temporary)
        restored = Path(temporary) / ROOT.name
        for name, digest in expected.items():
            assert sha(restored / name) == digest, name
        shutil.move(str(restored), str(ROOT))

receipt_path = Path("/content/ATML_PA2_setup_receipt.json")
receipt_bytes = (SAVED / receipt_path.name).read_bytes()
assert not receipt_path.exists() or receipt_path.read_bytes() == receipt_bytes
receipt_path.write_bytes(receipt_bytes)
EVIDENCE = Path(json.loads(receipt_bytes)["backup_directory"])
assert EVIDENCE.is_dir()

BACKUP = Path(bundle["training_backup_root"])
assert BACKUP == Path(
    "/content/drive/MyDrive/ATML-Assignment-2/training_runs"
)
BACKUP.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

standard = BACKUP / "standard_20261004_143635_293670_COMPLETE_RELOAD_VERIFIED"
assert sha(standard / "run_record.json") == bundle["standard_reference_record_sha256"]
reference = json.loads((standard / "run_record.json").read_text())
assert reference["status"] == "COMPLETE_RELOAD_VERIFIED"
assert reference["effective_config"] == bundle["effective_config"]
assert reference["scaler_settings"] == bundle["scaler_settings"]
assert bundle["scaler_settings"] == {
    "enabled": True, "init_scale": 1024.,
    "growth_factor": 2., "backoff_factor": .5, "growth_interval": 2000,
}

for job in bundle["jobs"]:
    name = job["condition"]
    pairs = 1500 if name == "length_balanced" else 600
    beta = {
        "beta_003": .03, "beta_010": .10,
        "beta_030": .30, "length_balanced": .10,
    }[name]
    assert (
        job["pairs"], job["epochs"], job["beta"], job["initial_gradient_scale"]
    ) == (pairs, 1, beta, 1024.)
    assert job["update_pair_counts"] == [
        min(16, pairs - i) for i in range(0, pairs, 16)
    ]
    assert job["optimizer_updates"] == len(job["update_pair_counts"])
    assert not Path(job["output"]).exists(), (
        "Training output exists; preserve it and paste the output."
    )
    assert not [
        p for p in BACKUP.glob(f"{name}_*") if p.is_dir()
    ], "A comparison attempt exists; paste the output before rerunning."

    args = [
        "-m", "task1_dpo.train", "--config", "configs/dpo.yaml",
        "--run-name", name, "--models-dir", bundle["local_models_dir"],
        "--output", job["output"], "--backup-root", str(BACKUP),
    ]
    assert job["command_arguments"] == args

stamp = datetime.now(ZoneInfo("Asia/Karachi")).strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S_%f")
STAGE = ROOT / "results/setup" / ("gpu_four_comparison_training_" + stamp)
STAGE.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=False)
record = {
    "status": "PREPARING", "prepared_jobs": str(PREP), "bundle": bundle,
    "ML_settings_changed": False, "training_invoked": False,
    "completed_jobs": [], "initial_gradient_scale": 1024.,
    "gpu": torch.cuda.get_device_name(0), "torch": str(torch.__version__),
}
started = time.perf_counter()

try:
    shutil.copyfile(
        PREP / "comparison_training_jobs.json", STAGE / "prepared_jobs.json"
    )
    shutil.copyfile(
        PREP / "standard_sft_complete_results.json",
        STAGE / "standard_sft_complete_results.json"
    )
    shutil.copyfile(
        ROOT / "docs/task1_gradient_scale1024.md",
        STAGE / "theory_and_change.md"
    )

    # Restore mismatched packages while preserving GPU PyTorch.
    pins = {
        "transformers": "4.57.1", "tokenizers": "0.22.1",
        "peft": "0.17.1", "trl": "0.27.2", "bitsandbytes": "0.50.2",
    }
    constraints = STAGE / "constraints.txt"
    constraints.write_text(
        "\n".join(f"{name}=={version}" for name, version in pins.items())
        + "\ntorch==2.11.0+cu130\nhuggingface_hub<1\n"
    )
    requirements = [
        Requirement(line.split("#", 1)[0].strip())
        for line in (ROOT / "requirements.txt").read_text().splitlines()
        if line.split("#", 1)[0].strip()
    ]
    requirements += [
        Requirement(f"{name}=={version}") for name, version in pins.items()
    ]
    requirements += [Requirement("huggingface_hub>=0.27,<1")]

    def mismatches():
        missing = []
        for requirement in requirements:
            if requirement.marker and not requirement.marker.evaluate():
                continue
            try:
                installed = metadata.version(requirement.name)
            except metadata.PackageNotFoundError:
                missing.append(str(requirement))
                continue
            if installed not in requirement.specifier:
                missing.append(f"{requirement}: found {installed}")
        return sorted(set(missing))

    problems = mismatches()
    record["package_restoration_needed"] = bool(problems)
    if problems:
        print("Restoring pinned packages:", flush=True)
        print("\n".join(problems), flush=True)
        install = subprocess.run(
            [
                sys.executable, "-m", "pip", "install",
                "-r", str(ROOT / "requirements.txt"),
                "-c", str(constraints),
            ],
            cwd=ROOT, text=True, capture_output=True,
        )
        (STAGE / "installation.log").write_text(
            install.stdout + "\n" + install.stderr
        )
        if install.returncode:
            print("\n".join(
                (install.stdout + install.stderr).splitlines()[-40:]
            ))
            raise RuntimeError("Package restoration failed; paste the output.")
    else:
        print("Pinned packages match; installation skipped.", flush=True)
        (STAGE / "installation.log").write_text(
            "Skipped: required versions already installed.\n"
        )
    assert not mismatches(), mismatches()
    assert metadata.version("torch") == "2.11.0+cu130"
    record["packages"] = {name: metadata.version(name) for name in pins}

    # Restore policy files once; every run loads a fresh model.
    MODELS = Path(bundle["local_models_dir"])
    SOURCE_MODELS = Path(bundle["drive_models_dir"])
    manifest_path = SOURCE_MODELS / "model_manifest.json"
    assert sha(manifest_path) == bundle["model_manifest_sha256"]
    model_manifest = json.loads(manifest_path.read_text())
    policy = model_manifest["policy"]
    assert (policy["model_id"], policy["revision"]) == (
        "Qwen/Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct",
        "989aa7980e4cf806f80c7fef2b1adb7bc71aa306",
    )
    source = (SOURCE_MODELS / policy["directory"]).resolve()
    destination = (MODELS / policy["directory"]).resolve()
    assert SOURCE_MODELS.resolve() in source.parents
    assert MODELS.resolve() in destination.parents
    destination.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    print("Restoring saved policy files; no model download...", flush=True)
    for index, (name, digest) in enumerate(policy["sha256"].items(), 1):
        origin = (source / name).resolve()
        target = (destination / name).resolve()
        assert source in origin.parents and destination in target.parents
        if not target.exists():
            target.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            shutil.copyfile(origin, target)
        assert sha(target) == digest, f"Policy file differs: {name}"
        print(f"Policy file {index}/{len(policy['sha256'])}: {name}", flush=True)

    local_manifest = MODELS / "model_manifest.json"
    if not local_manifest.exists():
        shutil.copyfile(manifest_path, local_manifest)
    assert sha(local_manifest) == bundle["model_manifest_sha256"]

    environment = dict(
        os.environ, PYTHONUNBUFFERED="1", HF_HUB_OFFLINE="1"
    )
    for job in bundle["jobs"]:
        name = job["condition"]
        record.update({
            "status": "TRAINING_RUNNING", "training_invoked": True,
            "current_condition": name,
        })
        (STAGE / "launch_record.json").write_text(
            json.dumps(record, indent=2)
        )
        print(
            f"STARTING FRESH {name.upper()}: {job['pairs']} pairs; "
            f"beta {job['beta']}; {job['optimizer_updates']} updates; scale 1024.",
            flush=True,
        )

        with (STAGE / f"{name}_console.log").open(
            "w", encoding="utf-8"
        ) as log:
            process = subprocess.Popen(
                [sys.executable, "-u", *job["command_arguments"]],
                cwd=ROOT, env=environment,
                stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
                text=True, bufsize=1, start_new_session=True,
            )
            try:
                for line in process.stdout:
                    print(line, end="", flush=True)
                    log.write(line)
                    log.flush()
                code = process.wait()
            except BaseException:
                if process.poll() is None:
                    os.killpg(process.pid, signal.SIGINT)
                    process.wait()
                raise

        assert code == 0, (
            f"{name} training failed; paste output. No automatic retry or continuation."
        )
        output = Path(job["output"])
        trained = json.loads((output / "run_record.json").read_text())
        reload_check = json.loads((output / "reload_verification.json").read_text())

        assert trained["status"] == "COMPLETE_RELOAD_VERIFIED"
        assert (
            trained["completed_pairs"], trained["completed_updates"]
        ) == (job["pairs"], job["optimizer_updates"])
        assert trained["plan"]["name"] == name
        assert trained["plan"]["beta"] == job["beta"]
        assert trained["selected_prompt_ids"] == job["selected_prompt_ids"]
        assert trained["plan"]["dataset_sha256"] == job["dataset_sha256"]
        assert trained["training"]["update_pair_counts"] == job["update_pair_counts"]
        assert trained["effective_config"] == bundle["effective_config"]
        assert trained["scaler_settings"] == bundle["scaler_settings"]
        assert trained["initial_adapter_sha256"] == reference["initial_adapter_sha256"]
        assert (
            trained["frozen_base_before_sha256"]
            == trained["frozen_base_after_sha256"]
            == reference["frozen_base_before_sha256"]
        )
        assert all(
            expected[relative] == digest
            for relative, digest in trained["source_sha256"].items()
        )
        assert reload_check["status"] == "PASS"
        assert reload_check["held_out_examples_used"] is False

        backup_name = f"{name}_{trained['started_at']}_COMPLETE_RELOAD_VERIFIED"
        receipt = json.loads(
            (BACKUP / (backup_name + "_backup_receipt.json")).read_text()
        )
        assert receipt["status"] == "BACKUP_VERIFIED"
        saved = Path(receipt["destination"])
        assert saved == BACKUP / backup_name
        core = (
            "run_record.json", "reload_verification.json",
            "adapter_config.json", "adapter_model.safetensors",
        )
        hashes = {filename: sha(saved / filename) for filename in core}
        for filename, digest in hashes.items():
            assert (
                digest == sha(output / filename)
                == receipt["sha256"][filename]
            )

        record["completed_jobs"].append({
            "condition": name, "status": trained["status"],
            "verified_backup": str(saved), "checkpoint_file_sha256": hashes,
            "completed_pairs": trained["completed_pairs"],
            "completed_updates": trained["completed_updates"],
        })
        (STAGE / "launch_record.json").write_text(
            json.dumps(record, indent=2)
        )
        print(json.dumps({
            "condition": name,
            "pairs": trained["completed_pairs"],
            "updates": trained["completed_updates"],
            "training_minutes": trained["training_wall_seconds"] / 60,
            "training_mean_loss": trained["training"]["training_mean_loss"],
            "training_preference_accuracy":
                trained["training"]["training_preference_accuracy"],
            "metrics_scope": "Training diagnostics only; held-out evaluation pending.",
            "fresh_initialization_matches_standard": True,
            "checkpoint_backup": str(saved),
        }, indent=2), flush=True)
        print(f"{name.upper()} CHECKPOINT RELOAD AND BACKUP VERIFIED.", flush=True)

    record["status"] = "FOUR_COMPARISON_TRAINING_RUNS_COMPLETE"

except BaseException as error:
    record["status"] = "FAILED"
    record["error"] = f"{type(error).__name__}: {error}"
    raise

finally:
    record["launcher_wall_seconds"] = time.perf_counter() - started
    (STAGE / "launch_record.json").write_text(json.dumps(record, indent=2))
    copied = EVIDENCE / STAGE.name
    hashes = {
        str(p.relative_to(STAGE)): sha(p)
        for p in STAGE.rglob("*") if p.is_file()
    }
    shutil.copytree(STAGE, copied)
    assert hashes == {
        str(p.relative_to(copied)): sha(p)
        for p in copied.rglob("*") if p.is_file()
    }, "Launcher backup failed; keep runtime connected."
    print("Launch evidence saved and verified:", copied, flush=True)
    print("Flushing Drive writes before GPU release...", flush=True)
    drive.flush_and_unmount(timeout_ms=120000)
    print("DRIVE_WRITES_FLUSHED", flush=True)

print("FOUR_COMPARISON_TRAINING_RUNS_COMPLETE")
for result in record["completed_jobs"]:
    print(result["condition"], "saved:", result["verified_backup"])
print("Save notebook; disconnect/delete GPU; paste the complete output.")

# CPU only: verify completed checkpoints and prepare held-out evaluations.
import hashlib
import json
from datetime import datetime
from pathlib import Path
from zoneinfo import ZoneInfo
import torch
from google.colab import drive

assert not torch.cuda.is_available(), "Select CPU before running this block."
drive.mount("/content/drive")

BASE = Path("/content/drive/MyDrive/ATML-Assignment-2")
EVIDENCE = BASE / "setup_evidence/20261003_161419_807248"
PREP = EVIDENCE / "reward_complete_comparison_jobs_20261004_171524_857140"
LAUNCH = EVIDENCE / "gpu_four_comparison_training_20261004_172413_595406"


def sha(path):
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    with Path(path).open("rb") as handle:
        for chunk in iter(lambda: handle.read(2**20), b""):
            digest.update(chunk)
    return digest.hexdigest()


def save_json(path, value):
    payload = json.dumps(value, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False, allow_nan=False)
    path.write_text(payload, encoding="utf-8")
    assert path.read_text(encoding="utf-8") == payload


for name, digest in json.loads((PREP / "evidence_sha256.json").read_text()).items():
    assert sha(PREP / name) == digest, name

previous = json.loads((PREP / "comparison_training_jobs.json").read_text())
launch = json.loads((LAUNCH / "launch_record.json").read_text())
assert launch["status"] == "FOUR_COMPARISON_TRAINING_RUNS_COMPLETE"
assert launch["bundle"] == previous
assert [j["condition"] for j in launch["completed_jobs"]] == [
    "beta_003", "beta_010", "beta_030", "length_balanced"
]
plans = {job["condition"]: job for job in previous["jobs"]}

standard = BASE / "training_runs/standard_20261004_143635_293670_COMPLETE_RELOAD_VERIFIED"
assert sha(standard / "run_record.json") == previous["standard_reference_record_sha256"]
reference = json.loads((standard / "run_record.json").read_text())

stamp = datetime.now(ZoneInfo("Asia/Karachi")).strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S_%f")
jobs, summaries = [], {}

for completed in launch["completed_jobs"]:
    name = completed["condition"]
    plan = plans[name]
    folder = Path(completed["verified_backup"])
    assert folder.parent == BASE / "training_runs"

    receipt = json.loads(
        folder.with_name(folder.name + "_backup_receipt.json").read_text()
    )
    assert receipt["status"] == "BACKUP_VERIFIED"
    assert Path(receipt["destination"]) == folder
    hashes = completed["checkpoint_file_sha256"]
    assert set(hashes) == {
        "run_record.json", "reload_verification.json",
        "adapter_config.json", "adapter_model.safetensors"
    }
    for filename, digest in hashes.items():
        assert sha(folder / filename) == digest == receipt["sha256"][filename]

    trained = json.loads((folder / "run_record.json").read_text())
    reload_check = json.loads((folder / "reload_verification.json").read_text())
    assert trained["status"] == "COMPLETE_RELOAD_VERIFIED"
    assert (
        trained["completed_pairs"], trained["completed_updates"]
    ) == (plan["pairs"], plan["optimizer_updates"])
    assert trained["plan"]["name"] == name
    assert trained["plan"]["beta"] == plan["beta"]
    assert trained["selected_prompt_ids"] == plan["selected_prompt_ids"]
    assert trained["training"]["update_pair_counts"] == plan["update_pair_counts"]
    assert trained["effective_config"] == previous["effective_config"]
    assert trained["scaler_settings"] == previous["scaler_settings"]
    assert trained["initial_adapter_sha256"] == reference["initial_adapter_sha256"]
    assert (
        trained["frozen_base_before_sha256"]
        == trained["frozen_base_after_sha256"]
        == reference["frozen_base_before_sha256"]
    )
    assert reload_check["status"] == "PASS"
    assert reload_check["held_out_examples_used"] is False

    summaries[name] = {
        "pairs": trained["completed_pairs"],
        "updates": trained["completed_updates"],
        "training_minutes": trained["training_wall_seconds"] / 60,
        "training_mean_loss": trained["training"]["training_mean_loss"],
        "training_preference_accuracy":
            trained["training"]["training_preference_accuracy"],
        "metrics_scope": "Training diagnostics only; held-out evaluation pending.",
        "checkpoint": str(folder),
        "checkpoint_file_sha256": hashes,
    }

    pair_pools = {"dpo_standard_eval": 300}
    generation_pools = {"dpo_standard_eval": 300}
    if name == "length_balanced":
        pair_pools["dpo_length_eval"] = 246
        generation_pools["word_limit_prompts"] = 10

    adapter = f"/content/ATML_PA2_adapters/{name}_completed_scale1024"
    output = f"/content/ATML-Assignment-2/results/task1_dpo/{name}/policy_{stamp}"
    jobs.append({
        "condition": name,
        "phase": "policy",
        "output": output,
        "pair_pools": pair_pools,
        "generation_pools": generation_pools,
        "completed_checkpoint": str(folder),
        "checkpoint_file_sha256": hashes,
        "local_adapter": adapter,
        "command_arguments": [
            "-m", "task1_dpo.evaluate", "--config", "configs/dpo.yaml",
            "--name", name, "--phase", "policy",
            "--models-dir", previous["local_models_dir"],
            "--adapter", adapter, "--output", output,
            "--backup-root", str(BASE / "evaluation_runs"),
        ],
    })
    print(
        f"{name}: checkpoint verified; evaluation "
        f"{sum(pair_pools.values())} pairs + "
        f"{sum(generation_pools.values())} answers."
    )

bundle = {
    key: previous[key] for key in (
        "project_backup", "project_archive_sha256",
        "drive_models_dir", "local_models_dir", "model_manifest_sha256",
    )
}
bundle.update({
    "status": "PREPARED_FOUR_COMPARISON_POLICY_EVALUATIONS_NOT_STARTED",
    "jobs": jobs,
    "evaluation_backup_root": str(BASE / "evaluation_runs"),
    "effective_config": previous["effective_config"],
    "ML_settings_changed": False,
    "training_launch_evidence": str(LAUNCH),
    "training_launch_record_sha256": sha(LAUNCH / "launch_record.json"),
    "scope":
        "Existing evaluator, fixed prompts/decoding/seed; final full-budget "
        "checkpoints; no held-out model selection. "
        "Reward scoring of saved answers follows.",
})

STAGE = EVIDENCE / ("comparison_complete_evaluation_jobs_" + stamp)
STAGE.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=False)
save_json(STAGE / "comparison_training_completion.json", summaries)
save_json(STAGE / "comparison_policy_evaluation_jobs.json", bundle)
save_json(STAGE / "evidence_sha256.json", {
    name: sha(STAGE / name)
    for name in (
        "comparison_training_completion.json",
        "comparison_policy_evaluation_jobs.json",
    )
})

print("COMPARISON_CHECKPOINTS_AND_EVALUATION_JOBS_OK")
print("All five DPO training conditions are complete; these four evaluations are pending.")
print("Prepared: 1446 held-out pairs and 1210 generated answers across four models.")
print("Evidence saved and verified:", STAGE)
print("Keep CPU selected. No weights loaded, evaluation started, or settings changed.")

from pathlib import Path, PurePosixPath
from datetime import datetime
from zoneinfo import ZoneInfo
from importlib import metadata
from pip._vendor.packaging.requirements import Requirement
import hashlib, json, os, shutil, signal, stat
import subprocess, sys, tempfile, time, zipfile
import torch
from google.colab import drive

print("PyTorch:", torch.__version__, "CUDA:", torch.version.cuda, flush=True)
assert torch.cuda.is_available(), "Select A100 GPU, save, and reconnect first."
print("GPU:", torch.cuda.get_device_name(0), flush=True)
assert "A100" in torch.cuda.get_device_name(0)
assert torch.cuda.get_device_properties(0).total_memory / 2**30 >= 39
assert str(torch.__version__) == "2.11.0+cu130" and torch.version.cuda == "13.0", (
    "GPU environment differs; paste the output."
)
drive.mount("/content/drive")

ROOT = Path("/content/ATML-Assignment-2")
PREP = Path(
    "/content/drive/MyDrive/ATML-Assignment-2/"
    "setup_evidence/20261003_161419_807248/"
    "comparison_complete_evaluation_jobs_20261004_175600_525886"
)


def sha(path):
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    with Path(path).open("rb") as handle:
        for chunk in iter(lambda: handle.read(2**20), b""):
            digest.update(chunk)
    return digest.hexdigest()


for name, digest in json.loads((PREP / "evidence_sha256.json").read_text()).items():
    assert sha(PREP / name) == digest, f"Prepared evidence changed: {name}"

bundle = json.loads((PREP / "comparison_policy_evaluation_jobs.json").read_text())
assert bundle["status"] == "PREPARED_FOUR_COMPARISON_POLICY_EVALUATIONS_NOT_STARTED"
assert bundle["ML_settings_changed"] is False
assert [j["condition"] for j in bundle["jobs"]] == [
    "beta_003", "beta_010", "beta_030", "length_balanced"
]

SAVED = Path(bundle["project_backup"])
assert SAVED == Path(
    "/content/drive/MyDrive/ATML-Assignment-2/"
    "runtime_backups/20261004_142324_585826"
)
manifest = json.loads((SAVED / "backup_manifest.json").read_text())
assert manifest["status"] == "BACKUP_VERIFIED"
assert manifest["archive_root"] == ROOT.name
assert manifest["archive_sha256"] == bundle["project_archive_sha256"]
expected = manifest["file_sha256"]
assert len(expected) == 279
assert set(expected) == set(manifest["included_files"])
assert sha(SAVED / "project.zip") == manifest["archive_sha256"]

print("Restoring verified evaluation code/data...", flush=True)
if ROOT.exists():
    differences = [
        n for n, d in expected.items()
        if not (ROOT / n).is_file() or sha(ROOT / n) != d
    ]
    assert not differences, (
        f"Existing project differs; preserve it and paste: {differences[:10]}"
    )
else:
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(
        prefix="atml_eval_restore_", dir="/content"
    ) as temporary:
        with zipfile.ZipFile(SAVED / "project.zip") as archive:
            entries = archive.infolist()
            names = {
                str(PurePosixPath(ROOT.name) / name) for name in expected
            }
            assert len(entries) == len(names)
            assert {e.filename for e in entries} == names
            for entry in entries:
                path = PurePosixPath(entry.filename)
                assert not path.is_absolute() and ".." not in path.parts
                assert path.parts[0] == ROOT.name and not entry.is_dir()
                assert not stat.S_ISLNK(entry.external_attr >> 16)
            archive.extractall(temporary)
        restored = Path(temporary) / ROOT.name
        for name, digest in expected.items():
            assert sha(restored / name) == digest, name
        shutil.move(str(restored), str(ROOT))

receipt_path = Path("/content/ATML_PA2_setup_receipt.json")
receipt_bytes = (SAVED / receipt_path.name).read_bytes()
assert not receipt_path.exists() or receipt_path.read_bytes() == receipt_bytes
receipt_path.write_bytes(receipt_bytes)
EVIDENCE = Path(json.loads(receipt_bytes)["backup_directory"])
assert EVIDENCE.is_dir()

BACKUP = Path(bundle["evaluation_backup_root"])
assert BACKUP == Path(
    "/content/drive/MyDrive/ATML-Assignment-2/evaluation_runs"
)
BACKUP.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

for job in bundle["jobs"]:
    name = job["condition"]
    assert job["phase"] == "policy"
    pairs = {"dpo_standard_eval": 300}
    generations = {"dpo_standard_eval": 300}
    if name == "length_balanced":
        pairs["dpo_length_eval"] = 246
        generations["word_limit_prompts"] = 10
    assert job["pair_pools"] == pairs
    assert job["generation_pools"] == generations
    assert not Path(job["output"]).exists(), (
        "Evaluation output exists; preserve it and paste the output."
    )
    assert not [
        p for p in BACKUP.glob(f"eval_{name}_policy_*") if p.is_dir()
    ], "An evaluation attempt exists; paste the output before rerunning."
    args = [
        "-m", "task1_dpo.evaluate", "--config", "configs/dpo.yaml",
        "--name", name, "--phase", "policy",
        "--models-dir", bundle["local_models_dir"],
        "--adapter", job["local_adapter"],
        "--output", job["output"], "--backup-root", str(BACKUP),
    ]
    assert job["command_arguments"] == args

stamp = datetime.now(ZoneInfo("Asia/Karachi")).strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S_%f")
STAGE = ROOT / "results/setup" / ("gpu_four_comparison_policy_" + stamp)
STAGE.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=False)
record = {
    "status": "PREPARING", "prepared_jobs": str(PREP), "bundle": bundle,
    "ML_settings_changed": False, "training_invoked": False,
    "evaluation_invoked": False, "completed_jobs": [],
    "gpu": torch.cuda.get_device_name(0), "torch": str(torch.__version__),
}
started = time.perf_counter()

try:
    shutil.copyfile(
        PREP / "comparison_policy_evaluation_jobs.json",
        STAGE / "prepared_jobs.json"
    )
    shutil.copyfile(
        PREP / "comparison_training_completion.json",
        STAGE / "comparison_training_completion.json"
    )

    # Restore mismatched packages while preserving GPU PyTorch.
    pins = {
        "transformers": "4.57.1", "tokenizers": "0.22.1",
        "peft": "0.17.1", "trl": "0.27.2", "bitsandbytes": "0.50.2",
    }
    constraints = STAGE / "constraints.txt"
    constraints.write_text(
        "\n".join(f"{name}=={version}" for name, version in pins.items())
        + "\ntorch==2.11.0+cu130\nhuggingface_hub<1\n"
    )
    requirements = [
        Requirement(line.split("#", 1)[0].strip())
        for line in (ROOT / "requirements.txt").read_text().splitlines()
        if line.split("#", 1)[0].strip()
    ]
    requirements += [
        Requirement(f"{name}=={version}") for name, version in pins.items()
    ]
    requirements += [Requirement("huggingface_hub>=0.27,<1")]

    def mismatches():
        missing = []
        for requirement in requirements:
            if requirement.marker and not requirement.marker.evaluate():
                continue
            try:
                installed = metadata.version(requirement.name)
            except metadata.PackageNotFoundError:
                missing.append(str(requirement))
                continue
            if installed not in requirement.specifier:
                missing.append(f"{requirement}: found {installed}")
        return sorted(set(missing))

    problems = mismatches()
    record["package_restoration_needed"] = bool(problems)
    if problems:
        print("Restoring pinned packages:", flush=True)
        print("\n".join(problems), flush=True)
        install = subprocess.run(
            [
                sys.executable, "-m", "pip", "install",
                "-r", str(ROOT / "requirements.txt"),
                "-c", str(constraints),
            ],
            cwd=ROOT, text=True, capture_output=True,
        )
        (STAGE / "installation.log").write_text(
            install.stdout + "\n" + install.stderr
        )
        if install.returncode:
            print("\n".join(
                (install.stdout + install.stderr).splitlines()[-40:]
            ))
            raise RuntimeError("Package restoration failed; paste the output.")
    else:
        print("Pinned packages match; installation skipped.", flush=True)
        (STAGE / "installation.log").write_text(
            "Skipped: required versions already installed.\n"
        )
    assert not mismatches(), mismatches()
    assert metadata.version("torch") == "2.11.0+cu130"
    record["packages"] = {name: metadata.version(name) for name in pins}

    # Restore the saved policy snapshot once.
    MODELS = Path(bundle["local_models_dir"])
    SOURCE_MODELS = Path(bundle["drive_models_dir"])
    manifest_path = SOURCE_MODELS / "model_manifest.json"
    assert sha(manifest_path) == bundle["model_manifest_sha256"]
    model_manifest = json.loads(manifest_path.read_text())
    policy = model_manifest["policy"]
    assert (policy["model_id"], policy["revision"]) == (
        "Qwen/Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct",
        "989aa7980e4cf806f80c7fef2b1adb7bc71aa306",
    )
    source = (SOURCE_MODELS / policy["directory"]).resolve()
    destination = (MODELS / policy["directory"]).resolve()
    assert SOURCE_MODELS.resolve() in source.parents
    assert MODELS.resolve() in destination.parents
    destination.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    print("Restoring saved policy files; no model download...", flush=True)
    for index, (name, digest) in enumerate(policy["sha256"].items(), 1):
        origin = (source / name).resolve()
        target = (destination / name).resolve()
        assert source in origin.parents and destination in target.parents
        if not target.exists():
            target.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            shutil.copyfile(origin, target)
        assert sha(target) == digest, f"Policy file differs: {name}"
        print(f"Policy file {index}/{len(policy['sha256'])}: {name}", flush=True)

    local_manifest = MODELS / "model_manifest.json"
    if not local_manifest.exists():
        shutil.copyfile(manifest_path, local_manifest)
    assert sha(local_manifest) == bundle["model_manifest_sha256"]

    # Restore each final adapter and its verified metadata.
    for job in bundle["jobs"]:
        name = job["condition"]
        checkpoint = Path(job["completed_checkpoint"]).resolve()
        adapter = Path(job["local_adapter"]).resolve()
        assert checkpoint.parent == Path(
            "/content/drive/MyDrive/ATML-Assignment-2/training_runs"
        )
        assert adapter == Path(
            f"/content/ATML_PA2_adapters/{name}_completed_scale1024"
        )
        adapter.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        assert set(job["checkpoint_file_sha256"]) == {
            "run_record.json", "reload_verification.json",
            "adapter_config.json", "adapter_model.safetensors",
        }
        print(f"Restoring completed {name} adapter...", flush=True)
        for filename, digest in job["checkpoint_file_sha256"].items():
            origin, target = checkpoint / filename, adapter / filename
            assert sha(origin) == digest, filename
            if not target.exists():
                shutil.copyfile(origin, target)
            assert sha(target) == digest, filename
        trained = json.loads((adapter / "run_record.json").read_text())
        assert trained["status"] == "COMPLETE_RELOAD_VERIFIED"
        assert trained["plan"]["name"] == name
        assert trained["effective_config"] == bundle["effective_config"]

    environment = dict(
        os.environ, PYTHONUNBUFFERED="1", HF_HUB_OFFLINE="1"
    )
    for job in bundle["jobs"]:
        name = job["condition"]
        record.update({
            "status": "EVALUATION_RUNNING", "evaluation_invoked": True,
            "current_condition": name,
        })
        (STAGE / "launch_record.json").write_text(
            json.dumps(record, indent=2)
        )
        print(
            f"STARTING {name.upper()} POLICY EVALUATION: "
            f"pairs={job['pair_pools']}; generation={job['generation_pools']}",
            flush=True,
        )
        with (STAGE / f"{name}_console.log").open(
            "w", encoding="utf-8"
        ) as log:
            process = subprocess.Popen(
                [sys.executable, "-u", *job["command_arguments"]],
                cwd=ROOT, env=environment,
                stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
                text=True, bufsize=1, start_new_session=True,
            )
            try:
                for line in process.stdout:
                    print(line, end="", flush=True)
                    log.write(line)
                    log.flush()
                code = process.wait()
            except BaseException:
                if process.poll() is None:
                    os.killpg(process.pid, signal.SIGINT)
                    process.wait()
                raise

        assert code == 0, (
            f"{name} evaluation failed; paste the output. Do not rerun automatically."
        )
        output = Path(job["output"])
        result = json.loads((output / "evaluation_record.json").read_text())
        assert result["status"] == "POLICY_COMPLETE"
        assert result["phase"] == "policy" and result["plan"]["name"] == name
        assert result["effective_config"] == bundle["effective_config"]
        assert Path(result["adapter"]) == Path(job["local_adapter"])
        assert result["checkpoint_file_sha256"] == job["checkpoint_file_sha256"]
        assert result["policy_parameters_unchanged"] is True

        metrics = json.loads((output / "metrics.json").read_text())
        assert set(metrics["pairs"]) == set(job["pair_pools"])
        assert set(metrics["generation"]) == set(job["generation_pools"])
        for key, count in job["pair_pools"].items():
            assert metrics["pairs"][key]["pairs"] == count
        for key, count in job["generation_pools"].items():
            assert metrics["generation"][key]["responses"] == count

        backup_name = f"eval_{name}_policy_{result['started_at']}_POLICY_COMPLETE"
        receipt = json.loads(
            (BACKUP / (backup_name + "_backup_receipt.json")).read_text()
        )
        assert receipt["status"] == "BACKUP_VERIFIED"
        saved = Path(receipt["destination"])
        assert saved == BACKUP / backup_name
        for filename in ("evaluation_record.json", "metrics.json"):
            assert (
                sha(saved / filename) == sha(output / filename)
                == receipt["sha256"][filename]
            )

        record["completed_jobs"].append({
            "condition": name, "phase": "policy", "status": "POLICY_COMPLETE",
            "verified_backup": str(saved),
            "evaluation_record_sha256": sha(saved / "evaluation_record.json"),
            "metrics_sha256": sha(saved / "metrics.json"),
        })
        (STAGE / "launch_record.json").write_text(
            json.dumps(record, indent=2)
        )
        concise = {
            "condition": name,
            "pairs": metrics["pairs"],
            "generation": {
                key: {
                    k: v for k, v in values.items()
                    if k not in {
                        "inherited_generation_config", "effective_generation_config"
                    }
                }
                for key, values in metrics["generation"].items()
            },
        }
        print(json.dumps(concise, indent=2), flush=True)
        print(f"{name.upper()} POLICY RESULTS VERIFIED:", saved, flush=True)

    record["status"] = "FOUR_COMPARISON_POLICY_EVALUATIONS_COMPLETE"

except BaseException as error:
    record["status"] = "FAILED"
    record["error"] = f"{type(error).__name__}: {error}"
    raise

finally:
    record["launcher_wall_seconds"] = time.perf_counter() - started
    (STAGE / "launch_record.json").write_text(json.dumps(record, indent=2))
    copied = EVIDENCE / STAGE.name
    hashes = {
        str(p.relative_to(STAGE)): sha(p)
        for p in STAGE.rglob("*") if p.is_file()
    }
    shutil.copytree(STAGE, copied)
    assert hashes == {
        str(p.relative_to(copied)): sha(p)
        for p in copied.rglob("*") if p.is_file()
    }, "Launcher backup failed; keep runtime connected."
    print("Launch evidence saved and verified:", copied, flush=True)
    print("Flushing Drive writes before GPU release...", flush=True)
    drive.flush_and_unmount(timeout_ms=120000)
    print("DRIVE_WRITES_FLUSHED", flush=True)

print("FOUR_COMPARISON_POLICY_EVALUATIONS_COMPLETE")
for result in record["completed_jobs"]:
    print(result["condition"], "saved:", result["verified_backup"])
print("Save notebook; disconnect/delete GPU; paste the complete output.")

# CPU only: verify saved comparison evaluations and prepare reward scoring.
import hashlib
import json
from datetime import datetime
from pathlib import Path
from zoneinfo import ZoneInfo
import torch
from google.colab import drive

assert not torch.cuda.is_available(), "Select CPU before running this block."
drive.mount("/content/drive")

BASE = Path("/content/drive/MyDrive/ATML-Assignment-2")
EVIDENCE = BASE / "setup_evidence/20261003_161419_807248"
PREP = EVIDENCE / "comparison_complete_evaluation_jobs_20261004_175600_525886"
LAUNCH = EVIDENCE / "gpu_four_comparison_policy_20261004_180508_319409"


def sha(path):
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    with Path(path).open("rb") as handle:
        for chunk in iter(lambda: handle.read(2**20), b""):
            digest.update(chunk)
    return digest.hexdigest()


def save_json(path, value):
    payload = json.dumps(value, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False, allow_nan=False)
    path.write_text(payload, encoding="utf-8")
    assert path.read_text(encoding="utf-8") == payload


for name, digest in json.loads((PREP / "evidence_sha256.json").read_text()).items():
    assert sha(PREP / name) == digest, name

previous = json.loads((PREP / "comparison_policy_evaluation_jobs.json").read_text())
launch = json.loads((LAUNCH / "launch_record.json").read_text())
assert launch["status"] == "FOUR_COMPARISON_POLICY_EVALUATIONS_COMPLETE"
assert launch["bundle"] == previous
assert launch["training_invoked"] is False
assert [j["condition"] for j in launch["completed_jobs"]] == [
    "beta_003", "beta_010", "beta_030", "length_balanced"
]
prepared_jobs = {j["condition"]: j for j in previous["jobs"]}

stamp = datetime.now(ZoneInfo("Asia/Karachi")).strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S_%f")
jobs, summaries = [], {}

for completed in launch["completed_jobs"]:
    name = completed["condition"]
    folder = Path(completed["verified_backup"])
    assert folder.parent == BASE / "evaluation_runs"

    receipt_path = folder.with_name(folder.name + "_backup_receipt.json")
    receipt = json.loads(receipt_path.read_text())
    assert receipt["status"] == "BACKUP_VERIFIED"
    assert Path(receipt["destination"]) == folder

    actual_files = {
        str(p.relative_to(folder)) for p in folder.rglob("*") if p.is_file()
    }
    assert actual_files == set(receipt["sha256"]), name
    print(f"Verifying saved {name} evaluation files...", flush=True)

    for relative, digest in receipt["sha256"].items():
        path = (folder / relative).resolve()
        assert folder.resolve() in path.parents
        assert sha(path) == digest, relative

    for filename, key in (
        ("evaluation_record.json", "evaluation_record_sha256"),
        ("metrics.json", "metrics_sha256"),
    ):
        assert receipt["sha256"][filename] == completed[key]

    record = json.loads((folder / "evaluation_record.json").read_text())
    metrics = json.loads((folder / "metrics.json").read_text())
    assert record["status"] == "POLICY_COMPLETE"
    assert record["phase"] == "policy"
    assert record["plan"]["name"] == metrics["name"] == name
    assert record["policy_parameters_unchanged"] is True
    assert all(
        receipt["sha256"][r] == d
        for r, d in record["artifact_sha256"].items()
    )
    assert record["effective_config"] == previous["effective_config"]
    assert (
        record["checkpoint_file_sha256"]
        == prepared_jobs[name]["checkpoint_file_sha256"]
    )

    expected_pairs = prepared_jobs[name]["pair_pools"]
    assert set(metrics["pairs"]) == set(expected_pairs)
    assert all(
        metrics["pairs"][key]["pairs"] == count
        for key, count in expected_pairs.items()
    )

    generations = prepared_jobs[name]["generation_pools"]
    assert set(metrics["generation"]) == set(generations)
    for key, count in generations.items():
        rows = [
            json.loads(line)
            for line in (folder / f"generation_{key}.jsonl").read_text().splitlines()
        ]
        assert len(rows) == metrics["generation"][key]["responses"] == count
        assert [r["row_index"] for r in rows] == list(range(count))
        assert [r["prompt_id"] for r in rows] == (
            record["plan"]["datasets"][key]["ordered_prompt_ids"]
        )

    summaries[name] = {
        "pairs": metrics["pairs"],
        "generation": {
            key: {
                k: v for k, v in values.items()
                if k not in {
                    "inherited_generation_config", "effective_generation_config"
                }
            }
            for key, values in metrics["generation"].items()
        },
    }

    local_results = f"/content/ATML_PA2_policy_results/{name}_completed"
    output = f"/content/ATML-Assignment-2/results/task1_dpo/{name}/reward_{stamp}"
    jobs.append({
        "condition": name,
        "phase": "reward",
        "output": output,
        "policy_results_drive": str(folder),
        "policy_results_local": local_results,
        "policy_results_sha256": receipt["sha256"],
        "generation_pools": generations,
        "command_arguments": [
            "-m", "task1_dpo.evaluate", "--config", "configs/dpo.yaml",
            "--name", name, "--phase", "reward",
            "--models-dir", previous["local_models_dir"],
            "--policy-results", local_results,
            "--output", output,
            "--backup-root", previous["evaluation_backup_root"],
        ],
    })

bundle = {
    key: previous[key] for key in (
        "project_backup", "project_archive_sha256",
        "drive_models_dir", "local_models_dir",
        "model_manifest_sha256", "evaluation_backup_root",
    )
}
bundle.update({
    "status": "PREPARED_REWARD_SCORING_NOT_STARTED",
    "jobs": jobs,
    "ML_settings_changed": False,
    "new_answers_generated": False,
    "reward_configuration_fix":
        "Previously approved in-memory rope_theta=1000000 compatibility correction.",
    "next_job_checks":
        "Existing evaluator verifies all complete reward inputs fit 4096 before "
        "loading reward weights; approved loader checks GPU placement and rotary settings.",
    "policy_launch_evidence": str(LAUNCH),
    "policy_launch_record_sha256": sha(LAUNCH / "launch_record.json"),
})

STAGE = EVIDENCE / ("comparison_policy_complete_reward_jobs_" + stamp)
STAGE.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=False)
save_json(STAGE / "policy_results_summary.json", summaries)
save_json(STAGE / "reward_evaluation_jobs.json", bundle)
save_json(STAGE / "evidence_sha256.json", {
    name: sha(STAGE / name)
    for name in ("policy_results_summary.json", "reward_evaluation_jobs.json")
})

print("COMPARISON_POLICY_RESULTS_AND_REWARD_JOBS_OK")
print("Verified: 1446 held-out pairs and 1210 saved answers across four models.")
print("Prepared reward scoring: three pools of 300 answers and one pool of 310.")
print("Evidence saved and verified:", STAGE)
print("Keep CPU selected. No weights loaded, answers regenerated, or settings changed.")

from pathlib import Path, PurePosixPath
from datetime import datetime
from zoneinfo import ZoneInfo
from importlib import metadata
from pip._vendor.packaging.requirements import Requirement
import hashlib, json, os, shutil, signal, stat
import subprocess, sys, tempfile, time, zipfile
import torch
from google.colab import drive

print("PyTorch:", torch.__version__, "CUDA:", torch.version.cuda, flush=True)
assert torch.cuda.is_available(), "Select A100 GPU, save, and reconnect first."
print("GPU:", torch.cuda.get_device_name(0), flush=True)
assert "A100" in torch.cuda.get_device_name(0)
assert torch.cuda.get_device_properties(0).total_memory / 2**30 >= 39
assert str(torch.__version__) == "2.11.0+cu130" and torch.version.cuda == "13.0", (
    "GPU environment differs; paste the output."
)
