    "/content/drive/MyDrive/ATML-Assignment-2/"
    "setup_evidence/20261003_161419_807248/"
    "comparison_policy_complete_reward_jobs_20261004_204232_870665"
)


def sha(path):
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    with Path(path).open("rb") as handle:
        for chunk in iter(lambda: handle.read(2**20), b""):
            digest.update(chunk)
    return digest.hexdigest()


for name, digest in json.loads((PREP / "evidence_sha256.json").read_text()).items():
    assert sha(PREP / name) == digest, f"Prepared evidence changed: {name}"

bundle = json.loads((PREP / "reward_evaluation_jobs.json").read_text())
assert bundle["status"] == "PREPARED_REWARD_SCORING_NOT_STARTED"
assert bundle["ML_settings_changed"] is False
assert [j["condition"] for j in bundle["jobs"]] == [
    "beta_003", "beta_010", "beta_030", "length_balanced"
]

SAVED = Path(bundle["project_backup"])
assert SAVED == Path(
    "/content/drive/MyDrive/ATML-Assignment-2/"
    "runtime_backups/20261004_142324_585826"
)
manifest = json.loads((SAVED / "backup_manifest.json").read_text())
assert manifest["status"] == "BACKUP_VERIFIED"
assert manifest["archive_root"] == ROOT.name
assert manifest["archive_sha256"] == bundle["project_archive_sha256"]
expected = manifest["file_sha256"]
assert len(expected) == 279
assert set(expected) == set(manifest["included_files"])
assert sha(SAVED / "project.zip") == manifest["archive_sha256"]

print("Restoring verified evaluation code/data...", flush=True)
if ROOT.exists():
    differences = [
        n for n, d in expected.items()
        if not (ROOT / n).is_file() or sha(ROOT / n) != d
    ]
    assert not differences, (
        f"Existing project differs; preserve it and paste: {differences[:10]}"
    )
else:
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(
        prefix="atml_eval_restore_", dir="/content"
    ) as temporary:
        with zipfile.ZipFile(SAVED / "project.zip") as archive:
            entries = archive.infolist()
            names = {
                str(PurePosixPath(ROOT.name) / name) for name in expected
            }
            assert len(entries) == len(names)
            assert {e.filename for e in entries} == names
            for entry in entries:
                path = PurePosixPath(entry.filename)
                assert not path.is_absolute() and ".." not in path.parts
                assert path.parts[0] == ROOT.name and not entry.is_dir()
                assert not stat.S_ISLNK(entry.external_attr >> 16)
            archive.extractall(temporary)
        restored = Path(temporary) / ROOT.name
        for name, digest in expected.items():
            assert sha(restored / name) == digest, name
        shutil.move(str(restored), str(ROOT))

receipt_path = Path("/content/ATML_PA2_setup_receipt.json")
receipt_bytes = (SAVED / receipt_path.name).read_bytes()
assert not receipt_path.exists() or receipt_path.read_bytes() == receipt_bytes
receipt_path.write_bytes(receipt_bytes)
EVIDENCE = Path(json.loads(receipt_bytes)["backup_directory"])
assert EVIDENCE.is_dir()

BACKUP = Path(bundle["evaluation_backup_root"])
assert BACKUP == Path(
    "/content/drive/MyDrive/ATML-Assignment-2/evaluation_runs"
)
BACKUP.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

for job in bundle["jobs"]:
    name = job["condition"]
    assert job["phase"] == "reward"
    generations = {"dpo_standard_eval": 300}
    if name == "length_balanced":
        generations["word_limit_prompts"] = 10
    assert job["generation_pools"] == generations
    assert not Path(job["output"]).exists(), (
        "Reward output exists; preserve it and paste the output."
    )
    assert not [
        p for p in BACKUP.glob(f"eval_{name}_reward_*") if p.is_dir()
    ], "A reward attempt exists; paste the output before rerunning."
    args = [
        "-m", "task1_dpo.evaluate", "--config", "configs/dpo.yaml",
        "--name", name, "--phase", "reward",
        "--models-dir", bundle["local_models_dir"],
        "--policy-results", job["policy_results_local"],
        "--output", job["output"], "--backup-root", str(BACKUP),
    ]
    assert job["command_arguments"] == args

stamp = datetime.now(ZoneInfo("Asia/Karachi")).strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S_%f")
STAGE = ROOT / "results/setup" / ("gpu_four_comparison_reward_" + stamp)
STAGE.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=False)
record = {
    "status": "PREPARING", "prepared_jobs": str(PREP), "bundle": bundle,
    "ML_settings_changed": False, "training_invoked": False,
    "evaluation_invoked": False, "new_answers_generated": False,
    "completed_jobs": [], "gpu": torch.cuda.get_device_name(0),
    "torch": str(torch.__version__),
}
started = time.perf_counter()

try:
    shutil.copyfile(
        PREP / "reward_evaluation_jobs.json", STAGE / "prepared_jobs.json"
    )
    shutil.copyfile(
        PREP / "policy_results_summary.json",
        STAGE / "policy_results_summary.json"
    )

    # Restore mismatched packages while preserving GPU PyTorch.
    pins = {
        "transformers": "4.57.1", "tokenizers": "0.22.1",
        "peft": "0.17.1", "trl": "0.27.2", "bitsandbytes": "0.50.2",
    }
    constraints = STAGE / "constraints.txt"
    constraints.write_text(
        "\n".join(f"{name}=={version}" for name, version in pins.items())
        + "\ntorch==2.11.0+cu130\nhuggingface_hub<1\n"
    )
    requirements = [
        Requirement(line.split("#", 1)[0].strip())
        for line in (ROOT / "requirements.txt").read_text().splitlines()
        if line.split("#", 1)[0].strip()
    ]
    requirements += [
        Requirement(f"{name}=={version}") for name, version in pins.items()
    ]
    requirements += [Requirement("huggingface_hub>=0.27,<1")]

    def mismatches():
        missing = []
        for requirement in requirements:
            if requirement.marker and not requirement.marker.evaluate():
                continue
            try:
                installed = metadata.version(requirement.name)
            except metadata.PackageNotFoundError:
                missing.append(str(requirement))
                continue
            if installed not in requirement.specifier:
                missing.append(f"{requirement}: found {installed}")
        return sorted(set(missing))

    problems = mismatches()
    record["package_restoration_needed"] = bool(problems)
    if problems:
        print("Restoring pinned packages:", flush=True)
        print("\n".join(problems), flush=True)
        install = subprocess.run(
            [
                sys.executable, "-m", "pip", "install",
                "-r", str(ROOT / "requirements.txt"),
                "-c", str(constraints),
            ],
            cwd=ROOT, text=True, capture_output=True,
        )
        (STAGE / "installation.log").write_text(
            install.stdout + "\n" + install.stderr
        )
        if install.returncode:
            print("\n".join(
                (install.stdout + install.stderr).splitlines()[-40:]
            ))
            raise RuntimeError("Package restoration failed; paste the output.")
    else:
        print("Pinned packages match; installation skipped.", flush=True)
        (STAGE / "installation.log").write_text(
            "Skipped: required versions already installed.\n"
        )
    assert not mismatches(), mismatches()
    assert metadata.version("torch") == "2.11.0+cu130"
    record["packages"] = {name: metadata.version(name) for name in pins}

    # Existing loader verifies both snapshots, but loads only reward weights.
    MODELS = Path(bundle["local_models_dir"])
    SOURCE_MODELS = Path(bundle["drive_models_dir"])
    manifest_path = SOURCE_MODELS / "model_manifest.json"
    assert sha(manifest_path) == bundle["model_manifest_sha256"]
    model_manifest = json.loads(manifest_path.read_text())
    identities = {
        "policy": (
            "Qwen/Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct",
            "989aa7980e4cf806f80c7fef2b1adb7bc71aa306",
        ),
        "reward": (
            "yavuz-ai/qwen2.5-1.5b-rm-ultrafeedback",
            "f98759a4a1ccdc47a3136748b6bfea9a32ba8fcc",
        ),
    }
    for kind, identity in identities.items():
        entry = model_manifest[kind]
        assert (entry["model_id"], entry["revision"]) == identity
        source = (SOURCE_MODELS / entry["directory"]).resolve()
        destination = (MODELS / entry["directory"]).resolve()
        assert SOURCE_MODELS.resolve() in source.parents
        assert MODELS.resolve() in destination.parents
        destination.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        print(f"Restoring saved {kind} files; no model download...", flush=True)
        for index, (name, digest) in enumerate(entry["sha256"].items(), 1):
            origin = (source / name).resolve()
            target = (destination / name).resolve()
            assert source in origin.parents and destination in target.parents
            if not target.exists():
                target.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
                shutil.copyfile(origin, target)
            assert sha(target) == digest, f"Prepared {kind} file differs: {name}"
            print(
                f"{kind} file {index}/{len(entry['sha256'])}: {name}",
                flush=True,
            )
    local_manifest = MODELS / "model_manifest.json"
    if not local_manifest.exists():
        shutil.copyfile(manifest_path, local_manifest)
    assert sha(local_manifest) == bundle["model_manifest_sha256"]

    # Restore complete saved policy results for reward replay.
    for job in bundle["jobs"]:
        source = Path(job["policy_results_drive"]).resolve()
        destination = Path(job["policy_results_local"]).resolve()
        assert source.parent == BACKUP.resolve()
        assert destination.parent == Path("/content/ATML_PA2_policy_results")
        if not destination.exists():
            shutil.copytree(source, destination)
        expected_results = job["policy_results_sha256"]
        assert set(expected_results) == {
            str(p.relative_to(destination))
            for p in destination.rglob("*") if p.is_file()
        }
        for relative, digest in expected_results.items():
            path = (destination / relative).resolve()
            assert destination in path.parents and sha(path) == digest, relative
        print(
            f"Saved {job['condition']} answers restored and verified.",
            flush=True,
        )

    environment = dict(
        os.environ, PYTHONUNBUFFERED="1", HF_HUB_OFFLINE="1"
    )
    for job in bundle["jobs"]:
        name = job["condition"]
        record.update({
            "status": "EVALUATION_RUNNING", "evaluation_invoked": True,
            "current_condition": name,
        })
        (STAGE / "launch_record.json").write_text(
            json.dumps(record, indent=2)
        )
        print(
            f"STARTING {name.upper()} REWARD SCORING: "
            f"{sum(job['generation_pools'].values())} saved answers.",
            flush=True,
        )
        with (STAGE / f"{name}_console.log").open(
            "w", encoding="utf-8"
        ) as log:
            process = subprocess.Popen(
                [sys.executable, "-u", *job["command_arguments"]],
                cwd=ROOT, env=environment,
                stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
                text=True, bufsize=1, start_new_session=True,
            )
            try:
                for line in process.stdout:
                    print(line, end="", flush=True)
                    log.write(line)
                    log.flush()
                code = process.wait()
            except BaseException:
                if process.poll() is None:
                    os.killpg(process.pid, signal.SIGINT)
                    process.wait()
                raise
        assert code == 0, (
            f"{name} scoring failed; paste output. Do not rerun automatically."
        )

        output = Path(job["output"])
        result = json.loads((output / "evaluation_record.json").read_text())
        assert result["status"] == "COMPLETE" and result["phase"] == "reward"
        assert result["plan"]["name"] == name
        assert result["complete_reward_inputs_verified"] is True

        metrics = json.loads((output / "metrics.json").read_text())
        audit = json.loads((output / "reward_input_audit.json").read_text())
        loading = json.loads((output / "reward_loading.json").read_text())
        assert loading["declared_theta"] == 1000000.0
        assert loading["original_file_unchanged"]
        assert loading["loaded_position_frequencies_match"]
        assert set(metrics["generation"]) == set(job["generation_pools"])

        concise = {"condition": name, "reward_scores": {}}
        for key, count in job["generation_pools"].items():
            values = metrics["generation"][key]
            assert values["responses"] == values["reward_score"]["count"] == count
            assert audit[key]["responses"] == count and audit[key]["truncated"] == 0
            assert audit[key]["maximum_complete_input_tokens"] <= 4096
            concise["reward_scores"][key] = values["reward_score"]

        backup_name = f"eval_{name}_reward_{result['started_at']}_COMPLETE"
        receipt = json.loads(
            (BACKUP / (backup_name + "_backup_receipt.json")).read_text()
        )
        assert receipt["status"] == "BACKUP_VERIFIED"
        saved = Path(receipt["destination"])
        assert saved == BACKUP / backup_name
        for filename in (
            "evaluation_record.json", "metrics.json",
            "reward_input_audit.json", "reward_loading.json",
        ):
            assert (
                sha(saved / filename) == sha(output / filename)
                == receipt["sha256"][filename]
            )

        record["completed_jobs"].append({
            "condition": name, "phase": "reward", "status": "COMPLETE",
            "verified_backup": str(saved),
            "evaluation_record_sha256": sha(saved / "evaluation_record.json"),
            "metrics_sha256": sha(saved / "metrics.json"),
        })
        (STAGE / "launch_record.json").write_text(
            json.dumps(record, indent=2)
        )
        print(json.dumps(concise, indent=2), flush=True)
        print(f"{name.upper()} REWARD RESULTS VERIFIED:", saved, flush=True)

    record["status"] = "FOUR_COMPARISON_REWARD_SCORING_COMPLETE"

except BaseException as error:
    record["status"] = "FAILED"
    record["error"] = f"{type(error).__name__}: {error}"
    raise

finally:
    record["launcher_wall_seconds"] = time.perf_counter() - started
    (STAGE / "launch_record.json").write_text(json.dumps(record, indent=2))
    copied = EVIDENCE / STAGE.name
    hashes = {
        str(p.relative_to(STAGE)): sha(p)
        for p in STAGE.rglob("*") if p.is_file()
    }
    shutil.copytree(STAGE, copied)
    assert hashes == {
        str(p.relative_to(copied)): sha(p)
        for p in copied.rglob("*") if p.is_file()
    }, "Launcher backup failed; keep runtime connected."
    print("Launch evidence saved and verified:", copied, flush=True)
    print("Flushing Drive writes before GPU release...", flush=True)
    drive.flush_and_unmount(timeout_ms=120000)
    print("DRIVE_WRITES_FLUSHED", flush=True)

print("FOUR_COMPARISON_REWARD_SCORING_COMPLETE")
for result in record["completed_jobs"]:
    print(result["condition"], "saved:", result["verified_backup"])
print("Save notebook; disconnect/delete GPU; paste the complete output.")

# CPU only: assemble the approved Task 1 tables from completed saved results.
import csv
import hashlib
import io
import json
from datetime import datetime
from pathlib import Path
from zoneinfo import ZoneInfo
import torch
from google.colab import drive

assert not torch.cuda.is_available(), "Select CPU before running this block."
drive.mount("/content/drive")
BASE = Path("/content/drive/MyDrive/ATML-Assignment-2")
EVIDENCE = BASE / "setup_evidence/20261003_161419_807248"


def sha(path):
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    with Path(path).open("rb") as handle:
        for chunk in iter(lambda: handle.read(2**20), b""):
            digest.update(chunk)
    return digest.hexdigest()


def save_json(path, value):
    payload = json.dumps(value, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False, allow_nan=False)
    path.write_text(payload, encoding="utf-8")
    assert path.read_text(encoding="utf-8") == payload


def save_csv(path, rows):
    buffer = io.StringIO(newline="")
    writer = csv.DictWriter(buffer, fieldnames=list(rows[0]))
    writer.writeheader()
    writer.writerows(rows)
    payload = buffer.getvalue()
    path.write_text(payload, encoding="utf-8", newline="")
    with path.open("r", encoding="utf-8", newline="") as handle:
        assert handle.read() == payload


def read_rows(path):
    return [json.loads(line) for line in path.read_text().splitlines()]


launches = (
    ("gpu_standard_sft_reward_20261004_165852_548773",
     "STANDARD_AND_SFT_REWARD_SCORING_COMPLETE", ["standard", "sft"]),
    ("gpu_four_comparison_reward_20261004_204919_086345",
     "FOUR_COMPARISON_REWARD_SCORING_COMPLETE",
     ["beta_003", "beta_010", "beta_030", "length_balanced"]),
)
metrics_by_name, sources, configuration = {}, {}, None
all_generations, cap_rows, word_review = {}, [], {}

for launch_name, status, names in launches:
    launch_path = EVIDENCE / launch_name / "launch_record.json"
    launch = json.loads(launch_path.read_text())
    assert launch["status"] == status
    assert launch["training_invoked"] is False and launch["new_answers_generated"] is False
    assert [j["condition"] for j in launch["completed_jobs"]] == names
    for completed in launch["completed_jobs"]:
        name = completed["condition"]
        folder = Path(completed["verified_backup"])
        assert folder.parent == BASE / "evaluation_runs"
        receipt = json.loads(folder.with_name(folder.name + "_backup_receipt.json").read_text())
        assert receipt["status"] == "BACKUP_VERIFIED" and Path(receipt["destination"]) == folder
        assert set(receipt["sha256"]) == {
            str(p.relative_to(folder)) for p in folder.rglob("*") if p.is_file()
        }
        print(f"Reading verified final results: {name}...", flush=True)
        for relative, digest in receipt["sha256"].items():
            path = (folder / relative).resolve()
            assert folder.resolve() in path.parents and sha(path) == digest, relative
        for filename, key in (
            ("evaluation_record.json", "evaluation_record_sha256"),
            ("metrics.json", "metrics_sha256"),
        ):
            assert receipt["sha256"][filename] == completed[key]

        record = json.loads((folder / "evaluation_record.json").read_text())
        metrics = json.loads((folder / "metrics.json").read_text())
        loading = json.loads((folder / "reward_loading.json").read_text())
        audit = json.loads((folder / "reward_input_audit.json").read_text())
        assert record["status"] == "COMPLETE" and record["phase"] == "reward"
        assert record["plan"]["name"] == metrics["name"] == name
        assert record["complete_reward_inputs_verified"]
        assert loading["declared_theta"] == 1000000.0
        assert loading["original_file_unchanged"] and loading["loaded_position_frequencies_match"]

        if configuration is None:
            configuration = record["effective_config"]
        assert configuration == record["effective_config"]

        expected_pairs = {} if name == "sft" else {"dpo_standard_eval": 300}
        generations = {"dpo_standard_eval": 300}
        if name in {"standard", "length_balanced"}:
            expected_pairs["dpo_length_eval"] = 246
        if name in {"standard", "length_balanced", "sft"}:
            generations["word_limit_prompts"] = 10
        assert set(metrics["pairs"]) == set(expected_pairs)
        assert set(metrics["generation"]) == set(generations)

        for key, count in expected_pairs.items():
            assert metrics["pairs"][key]["pairs"] == count

        for key, count in generations.items():
            generated = read_rows(folder / "policy_results" / f"generation_{key}.jsonl")
            rewards = read_rows(folder / f"rewards_{key}.jsonl")
            values = metrics["generation"][key]
            assert (
                len(generated) == len(rewards) == values["responses"]
                == values["reward_score"]["count"] == count
            )
            expected_ids = record["plan"]["datasets"][key]["ordered_prompt_ids"]
            assert [g["prompt_id"] for g in generated] == expected_ids
            assert audit[key]["responses"] == count and audit[key]["truncated"] == 0
            assert audit[key]["maximum_complete_input_tokens"] <= 4096

            merged = []
            for index, (g, r) in enumerate(zip(generated, rewards)):
                assert g["row_index"] == r["row_index"] == index
                assert g["prompt_id"] == r["prompt_id"]
                merged.append({**g, "reward_score": r["reward_score"]})
            all_generations[(name, key)] = merged

            cap_rows.append({
                "condition": name,
                "pool": key,
                "responses": count,
                "reached_256_token_ceiling_without_eos": sum(
                    g["reached_generation_ceiling"] for g in generated
                ),
                "terminated_with_eos": sum(g["terminated_with_eos"] for g in generated),
                "maximum_reward_input_tokens": audit[key]["maximum_complete_input_tokens"],
                "reward_input_truncations": 0,
            })

            if key == "word_limit_prompts":
                word_review[name] = [{
                    k: g[k] for k in (
                        "row_index", "prompt_id", "messages", "response",
                        "response_length", "word_count", "word_limit",
                        "word_limit_compliance", "reward_score",
                        "reached_generation_ceiling",
                    )
                } for g in merged]

        metrics_by_name[name] = metrics
        sources[name] = {
            "final_reward_folder": str(folder),
            "final_artifact_sha256": receipt["sha256"],
            "launch_record": str(launch_path),
            "launch_record_sha256": sha(launch_path),
        }

order = ["sft", "standard", "beta_003", "beta_010", "beta_030", "length_balanced"]
summary, strata, word_table = [], [], []

for name in order:
    metrics = metrics_by_name[name]
    pair = metrics["pairs"].get("dpo_standard_eval", {})
    g = metrics["generation"]["dpo_standard_eval"]
    summary.append({
        "condition": name,
        "training_pairs": (
            0 if name == "sft" else 600 if name.startswith("beta_") else 1500
        ),
        "training_epochs": 0 if name == "sft" else 1,
        "beta": metrics["beta"],
        "held_out_pairs": pair.get("pairs"),
        "dpo_loss": pair.get("mean_dpo_loss"),
        "preference_accuracy": pair.get("preference_accuracy"),
        "correct_pairs": pair.get("correct_pairs"),
        "ties": pair.get("ties"),
        "generated_responses": g["responses"],
        "sampled_kl_per_token": g["sampled_kl"],
        "reward_mean": g["reward_score"]["mean"],
        "reward_population_sd": g["reward_score"]["population_std"],
        "length_tokens_mean": g["response_length_tokens"]["mean"],
        "length_tokens_population_sd": g["response_length_tokens"]["population_std"],
    })

    if "dpo_length_eval" in metrics["pairs"]:
        for stratum, p in metrics["pairs"]["dpo_length_eval"]["strata"].items():
            strata.append({
                "condition": name,
                "stratum": stratum,
                "pairs": p["pairs"],
                "correct_pairs": p["correct_pairs"],
                "preference_accuracy": p["preference_accuracy"],
                "dpo_loss": p["mean_dpo_loss"],
                "ties": p["ties"],
            })

    if "word_limit_prompts" in metrics["generation"]:
        w = metrics["generation"]["word_limit_prompts"]
        word_table.append({
            "condition": name,
            "prompts": w["responses"],
            "compliant_prompts": sum(
                r["word_limit_compliance"] for r in word_review[name]
            ),
            "word_limit_compliance": w["word_limit_compliance"],
            "length_tokens_mean": w["response_length_tokens"]["mean"],
            "length_tokens_population_sd": w["response_length_tokens"]["population_std"],
            "length_words_mean": w["response_length_words"]["mean"],
            "length_words_population_sd": w["response_length_words"]["population_std"],
            "reward_mean": w["reward_score"]["mean"],
        })

stamp = datetime.now(ZoneInfo("Asia/Karachi")).strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S_%f")
STAGE = BASE / "analysis" / ("task1_quantitative_" + stamp)
STAGE.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=False)

for filename, rows in (
    ("summary.csv", summary),
    ("length_strata.csv", strata),
    ("word_limit_comparison.csv", word_table),
    ("generation_ceiling_audit.csv", cap_rows),
):
    save_csv(STAGE / filename, rows)

save_json(STAGE / "metrics.json", metrics_by_name)
save_json(STAGE / "provenance.json", {
    "status": "QUANTITATIVE_RESULTS_VERIFIED_QUALITATIVE_REVIEW_PENDING",
    "effective_config": configuration,
    "sources": sources,
    "metric_notes": [
        "Loss uses each condition's own beta; different beta losses are not interchangeable quality scores.",
        "Standard/balanced train1500 pairs; beta forks600; SFT is the untouched baseline.",
        "Sampled KL is signed global valid-response-token-weighted log-probability difference; negative estimates are preserved.",
        "Response-token length includes generated EOS; population SD; cap256 remains fixed.",
        "Word-limit compliance uses all10 fixed prompts and the unchanged course rule; no statistical significance claim.",
        "No training/model selection/settings changes; all figures and qualitative selections remain to be reviewed.",
    ],
})
save_json(STAGE / "word_limit_review.json", {
    "status": "UNSELECTED_SAVED_EXAMPLES_FOR_JOINT_REVIEW",
    "conditions": word_review,
})

for name in order:
    rows = all_generations[(name, "dpo_standard_eval")]
    save_json(STAGE / f"saved_general_responses_{name}.json", rows)

save_json(STAGE / "evidence_sha256.json", {
    p.name: sha(p) for p in STAGE.iterdir() if p.is_file()
})

print("\nTASK1_QUANTITATIVE_RESULTS_OK")
print("All six policies' completed results verified; required comparison tables saved.")
print(json.dumps({
    "summary": summary,
    "length_strata": strata,
    "word_limit_comparison": word_table,
    "generation_ceiling_audit": cap_rows,
}, indent=2, allow_nan=False))
print("Results and review records saved and verified:", STAGE)
print("GPU stays off. Qualitative review, report presentation and submission package remain.")

# CPU only: export existing Task 1 results and answers for joint review.
import hashlib
import json
import shutil
import zipfile
from datetime import datetime
from pathlib import Path
from zoneinfo import ZoneInfo

import torch
from google.colab import drive, files

assert not torch.cuda.is_available(), "Keep CPU selected for this block."
drive.mount("/content/drive")

BASE = Path("/content/drive/MyDrive/ATML-Assignment-2")
SOURCE = BASE / "analysis/task1_quantitative_20261004_214602_567835"
assert SOURCE.is_dir(), "Saved quantitative results folder is missing."


def sha(path):
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    with Path(path).open("rb") as handle:
        for chunk in iter(lambda: handle.read(2**20), b""):
            digest.update(chunk)
    return digest.hexdigest()


manifest_path = SOURCE / "evidence_sha256.json"
manifest = json.loads(manifest_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
assert set(manifest) == {
    p.name for p in SOURCE.iterdir()
    if p.is_file() and p.name != "evidence_sha256.json"
}
for name, expected in manifest.items():
    path = SOURCE / name
    assert path.parent == SOURCE and path.is_file()
    assert sha(path) == expected, f"Saved file changed: {name}"

stamp = datetime.now(ZoneInfo("Asia/Karachi")).strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S_%f")
LOCAL_ZIP = Path("/content") / f"Task1_review_bundle_{stamp}.zip"

with zipfile.ZipFile(
    LOCAL_ZIP, "w", compression=zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED
) as archive:
    for path in sorted(SOURCE.iterdir()):
        if path.is_file():
            archive.write(path, arcname=f"{SOURCE.name}/{path.name}")

EXPORT = BASE / "analysis_exports" / f"review_{stamp}"
EXPORT.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=False)
DRIVE_ZIP = EXPORT / LOCAL_ZIP.name
shutil.copy2(LOCAL_ZIP, DRIVE_ZIP)
archive_hash = sha(LOCAL_ZIP)
assert sha(DRIVE_ZIP) == archive_hash

receipt = {
    "status": "REVIEW_BUNDLE_SAVED_AND_VERIFIED",
    "source_analysis": str(SOURCE),
    "source_manifest_sha256": sha(manifest_path),
    "archive": str(DRIVE_ZIP),
    "archive_sha256": archive_hash,
    "includes": (
        "Comparison tables, provenance, all saved general answers, "
        "all word-limit answers."
    ),
    "qualitative_selection": "Pending joint review and user approval.",
    "new_training_or_generation": False,
    "settings_changed": False,
}
payload = json.dumps(receipt, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False, allow_nan=False)
receipt_path = EXPORT / "export_receipt.json"
receipt_path.write_text(payload, encoding="utf-8")
assert receipt_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8") == payload

print("TASK1_REVIEW_BUNDLE_OK")
print("Download size (MiB):", round(LOCAL_ZIP.stat().st_size / 2**20, 2))
print("Verified Drive copy:", DRIVE_ZIP)
print("Existing answers exported; no examples selected and no models loaded.")
print("Attach the downloaded ZIP in this chat for our qualitative review.")
files.download(str(LOCAL_ZIP))
